# KAZE VS. MULTIVERSE — MODULAR CHARACTER CANON

> Current logical owner; availability comes from `state/current_state.yaml`. Provenance does not override live canon.


### DANA — MATERIAL MECHANICS — COMMITTED s5

Dana remains pure Saiyan. These physical applications operate through his legitimately available body, Kahuhu, skill and supports. Before the Second Temporal Relaunch he legitimately earned the complete documented gamma/Saiyan sequence through **Big Kahuna, Super Saiyan Big Kahuna, Super Saiyan 2 Big Kahuna, Super Saiyan 3 Big Kahuna and KAHUNA SOVEREIGN**. KAHUNA SOVEREIGN later receded and Dana returned to his 8'10" gamma-mutated baseline before departure; all five earned states remain available under their mechanics. Only the post-Sovereign tree remains separately gated.

Committed additions:

- kinetic anchoring through stance, skeleton, Kahuhu and ground/support path;
- impact vectoring away from protected people/structures where physically possible;
- controlled shockwave shaping through strike direction/timing rather than consequence erasure;
- structural load-path perception for rescue/bracing/collapse response;
- rescue deceleration/catch mechanics that spread stopping force across time/distance;
- Kahuhu recoil/impact routing through gauntlet, forearm, shoulder, torso and stance;
- ground-preservation fighting through deliberate footing/strike geometry;
- gamma heat-management / metabolic-thermal shedding through the gamma biology historically earned in the first relaunch and carried into the second relaunch.

None grants immovability, perfect rescue, consequence immunity or Angel biology.

### DANA PEEK-A-BOO / KAHUHU BOXING

Dana's close-range fighting style includes a Mike Tyson-inspired **peek-a-boo boxing layer**, adapted to his actual size, Saiyan movement, Ki, Kahuhu and Protector-Precision. It adds no Tyson memories, identity, automatic matchup solution or new supernatural power.

Use a compact high guard; head movement/slips; bob-and-weave entries; explosive lateral steps and angle changes; short hooks, shovel hooks, uppercuts and body shots; rapid combinations; pressure after legitimately closing range; slip-to-counter, roll-to-hook and block-to-return transitions. Body attacks can disrupt posture, breath, stance or concentration where anatomy and contact support that result. Kahuhu concentrates impact through its existing load paths; Protector-Precision directs force where protection requires it. Guard coverage, range, footing, timing and resistance still matter.

Kinetic anchoring, deliberate footing and structural awareness support entries, guard recovery and counters; Kahuhu routes recoil/impact through forearms, shoulders, torso, stance and actual supporting terrain. These applications preserve the existing direct-contact, structural-understanding and protector-judgment requirements, not immovability or collateral immunity. Boxing supplements Dana's brute-powerhouse identity, Saiyan movement, Ki, Storm Oath and Protector-Precision; it does not replace them or make every fight boxing-only. Brute-force haymakers remain possible and matchup-dependent. Earned Big Kahuna and later gamma stages retain learned boxing intelligence. Only a legitimately impairing temporary state may degrade technique; transformation by itself does not make him a mindless wide-swinging brute.

### DANA COMPLETE COMBAT INTEGRATION

Dana's user-approved repertoire adds to—not replaces—his Saiyan movement, Ki, Kahuhu, Protector-Precision, Storm mechanics and earned gamma/Kahuna states. He remains a technically complete, physically explosive pressure fighter rather than a slow heavyweight, a character impersonation or a random move catalogue. There is no fabricated training history or mastery of every unlisted technique merely from a style label.

**Functional capability routing:** for consequential Dana action, use **problem -> STRIKING / GRAPPLING / PROTECTION / POWER-TRANSFORMATION -> currently available mechanics -> valid combination -> resistance -> Dana's actual choice -> consequence**. This is retrieval structure, not four powers or a move carousel. Transformations never erase learned technique unless a real impairing state does so.

- **STRIKING** retrieves peek-a-boo boxing plus the full accepted Muay Thai/Lethwei/Pradal Serey/dirty-boxing/Boyka-influenced kick-and-knee language, Ki/Kahuhu delivery and terrain-supported combinations.
- **GRAPPLING** retrieves Greco-Roman, Judo, Combat Sambo, catch wrestling, MMA transitions, submissions, wall/terrain wrestling, throws/slams and the approved pro-wrestling pool when geometry supports them.
- **PROTECTION** retrieves Protector-Precision, rescue deceleration, structural load-path work, interception, bracing, collateral-aware vectoring, body positioning and protection-driven aggression.
- **POWER / TRANSFORMATION** retrieves only forms, gamma escalation, Storm/Kahuhu and energy states actually available in current state; it modifies output and options without replacing STRIKING/GRAPPLING intelligence.

GEN-II must not default Dana to peek-a-boo boxing merely because it is visually memorable. A valid throw, kick, takedown, submission, pro-wrestling move, rescue mechanic or transformation answer may be the correct solution if position/objective/geometry support it. No repertoire item is owed screen time and no named wrestling move auto-finishes.

**Technical foundation:** retain peek-a-boo boxing; integrate Muay Thai, Lethwei and Pradal Serey striking/clinch work; dirty boxing; MMA takedowns/transitions and defensive positional knowledge; Greco-Roman upper-body wrestling; Judo reaps/sweeps/throws; Combat Sambo's mixed striking/throwing/submission approach; catch wrestling's rides/pins/front-headlocks; submission grappling; ground-and-pound; and wall/terrain wrestling. The accepted repertoire includes short punches/elbows/hammerfists, low/calf/round kicks/teeps/checks, knees/headbutts, underhooks/overhooks/body locks, singles/doubles/high-crotch lifts, mat returns, trips, hip throws, lateral drops, suplexes, grip/posture defense, escapes and purposeful standing back up. Specific named examples already locked are the armbar and rear-naked choke; other submission/leg-attack knowledge remains generic unless separately established. Technique knowledge does not guarantee injury or supply a real-world instruction sequence.

**Undisputed influence:** Scott Adkins/Yuri Boyka's screen-fighting language supplies explosive spinning/jumping kicks, hook/back/tornado/roundhouse/side/axe kicks, flying knees, deceptive kick transitions, alternating combinations, stance switches, acrobatic evasions, cartwheel/rotational transitions and floor recovery where Dana's current anatomy, footing and surroundings permit. Misses can transition into a new valid strike, clinch or takedown rather than resetting to a pose. No Boyka/Adkins identity, memories, dialogue, canonical record or stunt immunity transfers. Dana's actual size/momentum/support surfaces still matter.

**Explicit WWE/pro-wrestling pool:** **RKO; Pedigree; Batista Bomb; Tombstone Piledriver; Chokeslam; Spear; Jackhammer; Last Ride; 619; Samoan Drop; West Coast Pop; Hurricanrana**. Also retain the accepted broader clothesline/lariat, German/belly-to-belly/vertical suplex, powerbomb, spinebuster, DDT/piledriver, back-body-drop, powerslam/body-slam, running shoulder tackle, big boot, elbow/knee drop, overhead/military/gorilla press throw, swinging throw, corner splash and ground follow-through repertoire. These are real high-force techniques in fiction, not protected wrestling bumps. The airborne/headscissors and 619-type attacks require genuine control, usable geometry and suitable supports where the version needs them; arbitrary walls do not become ropes. Knowing a named move is not a screen-time obligation, ring requirement or automatic finisher. RKO/Pedigree/Batista Bomb are not fallback canned results for every clinch.

**Dirty fighting / anatomy targeting:** ear claps, headbutts, foot/shin/instep stomps, short palm/elbow/shoulder/forearm strikes, knee bumps, push-pull off-balancing, strikes with trips, clothing/armor/hair and applicable limb control, weapon-hand trapping, rough stand-up denial, pressure/pins, wall/terrain impacts, improvised pinning/crushing and deceptive rhythm or apparent sportsmanship where Dana would actually choose it. He may attack during an enemy reset, speech or transformation. The accepted target pool includes groin/dickshots, liver, kidneys, solar plexus, ribs/lower ribs, inside/outside thigh, calf, hip, shoulders, arms, gripping hands/wrists and already compromised areas. No sporting fairness restriction is imposed. Organ names, taps, a move's name or dirty intent do not guarantee a physiological response; compatible anatomy, delivery, force, leverage and resistance decide. This does not make all conduct obligatory or replace his protective judgment.

**Submission threshold / tap rule:** Dana deliberately committing to a punitive submission means the opponent has crossed a serious personal/protective threshold. Routine grappling, restraining, throwing or rescue control does not automatically cross it. A tap by itself **never causes release**. In that punitive state he continues toward his chosen endpoint: joint injury/incapacitation, unconsciousness or death when his actual anger/intent and circumstances support it. Dana releases because he decides his objective is complete or changes—not because a sports signal commands him. Resistance, interruption, changed urgent objectives, physiology and the possibility he cannot maintain or finish the hold remain real. No hold guarantees injury/death; no permission to impose this endpoint on every ordinary opponent.

### DANA — ENDGAME GAMMA-SAIYAN MUTATION

Shortly **before the MCU Battle of Earth fully begins**, during the immediate *Avengers: Endgame* sequence surrounding Hulk/Banner's Nano-Gauntlet Snap, Dana is exposed through legitimate Knockturnal seam/geography interaction to an abnormal Infinity-Stone-level gamma release associated with the Snap.

The exposure should be lethal. Dana's Saiyan physiology survives by permanently rewriting around the gamma load. The permanent gamma baseline rewrite and Dana's **first Big Kahuna manifestation are pre-war developments**, earned on screen before the Battle of Earth is fully underway. Later Super Saiyan/gamma stacks, SSJ2/SSJ3 progression, and Kahuna Sovereign remain later separately earned developments; they are not required before KNOCKTURNAL ends.

**Second-relaunch presentation correction:** the first gamma/Big Kahuna change remains causally real as historical first-relaunch development, but Dana now carries that earned progression into the Second Temporal Relaunch pilot opening. Do **not** replay the first mutation as a new acquisition when Banner's Snap repeats. If the old transformation is referenced, compress it as carried history; only genuinely new progression receives a new acquisition beat.

No Banner blood/tissue, Banner memories/personality, or copied Hulk identity is involved. Knockturnal itself does not arbitrarily hand Dana a power.

**Second Temporal Relaunch carryover:** this first-relaunch gamma rewrite/Big Kahuna progression is already earned and travels back with Dana. A repeated Banner Snap in the restored pilot timeline does not duplicate the mutation, create a second Big Kahuna stack or automatically advance Dana to SSJ2/SSJ3/Sovereign.

Dana gains a permanent gamma-mutated baseline plus a distinct gamma escalation state layered on his Saiyan system.

Baseline gains include improved resilience, regeneration, gamma tolerance, and latent gamma-linked strength scaling.

### BIG KAHUNA

Dana's official primary gamma escalation state is **BIG KAHUNA**.

Reference scale:
- height: approximately **13'1"**;
- weight: approximately **3,400-3,800 lb**, with ~3,600 lb a useful reference.

Physical direction:
- green skin;
- much wider shoulders;
- enormous traps;
- thicker neck;
- gigantic forearms and larger hands;
- expanded rib cage;
- huge thighs/calves;
- heavier brow/jaw;
- neck visually shortened by trap growth;
- slightly forward predator/gorilla resting posture when enraged;
- face remains Dana's face, gamma-expanded.

Early trigger routes include rage, severe pain, catastrophic injury, and extreme stress. Threats to someone Dana is protecting are the strongest trigger because Protector-Precision remains central.

Control model: **Protector Fury with a limited trigger-dependent gradient**. Gamma amplifies aggression, pain tolerance, and violent drive. Protection-triggered escalation is paradoxically more precise because Protector-Precision stays engaged. Raw rage/pain escalation is rougher and more collateral-prone.

Strength model: **Controlled Rage Scaling + Protector-Weighted Scaling**. Emotional escalation can increase strength, durability, healing, and density, but the scaling is state-dependent rather than infinite. Protective fury is the most efficient/stable route.

Regeneration model: **Escalating Gamma Regeneration + limited Adaptive Toughening**. Dana can heal wounds/fractures/organ trauma/blood loss/burns and handle radiation/compatible toxins better, with much stronger recovery in Big Kahuna. Healing costs resources; damage can outpace it. Repeated similar physical trauma may temporarily reinforce tissues/bones/connective structures during the same fight, but this is not permanent Kaze-style adaptation.

Kahuhu response: **gamma-forced expansion + battle-compatible wargear response**. The gauntlets enlarge/reinforce/thicken while keeping their recognizable forearm battering-ram / compact-fist identity.

Big Kahuna and every other active gamma/Kahuna transformation use a Thor: Ragnarok-Hulk-like core register: blunt, emotionally direct, physically grounded, strongly third-person-Dana phrasing with simplified grammar and natural variation. Dana remains fully Dana rather than becoming Banner/Hulk; intelligence, memory, technique and judgment stay intact. The detailed S39 speech section below controls all active gamma/Kahuna forms.

### SUPER SAIYAN / GAMMA PROGRESSION

All stages must be earned through natural physiological/emotional pressure: stress, protection, intense fights, pain/injury, escalating physical demand, genuine emotional pressure and adaptation. **There are no scheduled unlocks and no artificial pacing delays.** Once actual conditions satisfy the next threshold, Dana advances immediately; if successive thresholds are genuinely completed during one sustained crisis/battle, multiple stages may legitimately emerge in that same crisis. Historically, the permanent gamma baseline and Big Kahuna were not earned at the first pilot opening. Accepted pre-reset Paragon history subsequently completed **Big Kahuna -> Super Saiyan Big Kahuna -> Super Saiyan 2 Big Kahuna -> Super Saiyan 3 Big Kahuna -> KAHUNA SOVEREIGN**, and the Second Temporal Relaunch carries that full earned progression back to the pilot. These remain distinct states with their established mechanics; carrying them does not duplicate-stack a repeated Snap. Post-Sovereign branches remain future until earned. The initial Infinity-Stone gamma origin remains unchanged. A near-threshold event may fail when the actual conditions do not complete the transformation, but repeated near-threshold failure may not be used as an artificial pacing device after the threshold has genuinely been exceeded. No episode number or need for a larger fight supplies a missing condition.

Gamma-mutated Saiyan biology unlocks additional Saiyan stages previously inaccessible to Dana:
- Super Saiyan 2;
- Super Saiyan 3.

Gamma and Saiyan states initially remain separate enough to stack:
- Big Kahuna;
- **Super Saiyan Big Kahuna**;

- **Super Saiyan 3 Big Kahuna**.

Dana's dense/coiled Hawaiian hair **does not become classic spikes**. Transformations preserve the natural coiled texture and may change volume/lift/energy behavior, but **hair color remains black/deep-charcoal in every gamma-Saiyan stage and in Kahuna Sovereign**. No white, platinum, silver, or blonde transformation-hair state is active canon.

Super Saiyan Big Kahuna visual lock:

SSJ2 Big Kahuna and SSJ3 Big Kahuna also retain black/deep-charcoal natural coils. SSJ2 differentiates through more aggressive lift/volume and electrical pressure; SSJ3 differentiates through a dramatically longer, cascading **black coiled mane**, greater energy leakage, and greater biological strain rather than a hair-color change.

### KAHUNA SOVEREIGN

After SSJ3 progression, Dana's biology branches into a unique Dana-specific path rather than automatically copying God, Blue, Ultra Instinct, or GT Super Saiyan 4.

The first true fused phenotype is **KAHUNA SOVEREIGN**.

Kahuna Sovereign retains the S39 transformed third-person Dana/Hulk register while becoming calmer, more precise and better able to sequence complex decisions; **normal baseline vocabulary does not return while Sovereign is active**. Protector-Precision is stabilized without requiring rage. Stamina, control, efficiency, and sustainable output improve substantially. Kahuhu synchronizes directly with the fused body rather than merely resizing.

Big Kahuna is raw gamma conquest. Kahuna Sovereign is one stable gamma-Saiyan phenotype.

# 3. DANA
################################################################################

Species: pure Saiyan — LOCKED.
Dana has no second lineage or hybrid ancestry.

## DANA OFFICIAL VISUAL REFERENCE — CONTROLLING
The prior Dana image is not required by the deletion-safe cutover. **This section's textual Dana identity/form lock controls through current state gating.** At the Second Temporal Relaunch pilot opening Dana carries the permanent gamma rewrite and every legitimately earned gamma/Saiyan state through **KAHUNA SOVEREIGN**. Accepted departure evidence places him back at his 8'10" gamma-mutated baseline after Sovereign receded. Do not treat SSJ2/SSJ3/Sovereign as unearned; do not preload any **post-Sovereign** form merely because those future branches are documented. Dana remains pure Saiyan, **8'10" and 840 lbs** at baseline, extremely large and heavily muscular with functional proportions, mature/grounded/determined/battle-worn/protective facial identity, curly dark hair, beard with gray/white chin region, Polynesian visual influence, bare upper torso, layered cloth/rope waist, open sandals and lower-leg wraps. Tattoo placement/pattern is exact: RIGHT shoulder, RIGHT arm, RIGHT chest, and rear transition over RIGHT shoulder/upper back. NO MIRRORING, simplification or random alteration. Kahuhu retains massive forged gauntlets, forged metal, stone plating, bronze trim, ram-beast influence, segmented impact/knuckle construction, forearm connection and activated/heated orange glowing rune/seam presentation. Existing textual mechanics remain unchanged; the heated appearance grants no new mechanics. Dana descriptions do not control Mana; use Mana's dedicated textual section and current state.

Baseline character engine: working-class, conversational, practical, funny, argumentative, and willing to participate in chaos while complaining about it. He can roast, talk shit, tell stories, or start bullshit himself, but genuine danger flips him quickly into protector mode. Serious anger is blunt and practical; betrayal and threats to people he protects matter more than insults. Affection may be direct, action-based, or affectionate roasting. He respects competence, reliability, and backbone rather than titles. His development is on-screen: trust Kaze without blind following, accept command when needed, and evolve from trying to save everyone personally toward prioritization, delegation, and trusting others to make dangerous choices.

Tail history/status:
- Kaze told Dana the meta secret that Saiyans are stronger without their tails;
- Kaze removed Dana's tail;
- the tail biologically regrew once;
- Kaze cut it off a second time, after which it permanently stopped regrowing;
- why Kaze's second removal prevented regrowth has no further technical explanation: Kaze is simply capable of making the removal final.

Core combat identity: brute powerhouse.
Early fighting: raw force, overwhelming strength, close-range physical presence.
Developmental direction: Dana begins as a brute powerhouse and later unlocks a unique protector-precision transformation. The transformation is built around protecting others rather than surpassing an enemy, based on another meta secret revealed by Kaze.

"Angel-like elegance" describes the result but does NOT grant Angel biology, Ultra Instinct, divine status, God Ki, immortality, or Angel techniques.

## PROTECTOR-PRECISION TRANSFORMATION
Dana's body develops automatic combat precision through a unique transformation rather than Ultra Instinct. The precision governs his complete combat execution rather than only one isolated component.

Kaze's meta revelation changes Dana's evolutionary path by teaching that a Saiyan transformation can be constructed around protecting others rather than surpassing an enemy.

Unlock event: Dana must stop Kaze's leaking, universe-threatening power without hurting Kaze. The transformation permits Dana to redirect every particle of escaping energy beyond the universe's outer boundary into empty space.

After unlocking it, Dana may activate the form whenever he chooses. It requires no continuing sacrifice or drawback; the difficulty was unlocking it, not using it.

Moral role: protector.
Protection concern: any innocent person, not merely family/team; S36 explicitly qualifies action when Kaze targets them, including known innocents.

Baseline personality anchors:
- practical;
- hands-on;
- carpenter/repair competence remains a character anchor;
- friendly/joking/socially central outside serious conflict;
- physically intimidating but not automatically hostile;
- when genuinely pushed, can become quieter and more serious rather than merely louder.

The protector-precision form's exact name and visual design, Dana's remaining transformation tree, signature Ki techniques, and power ceiling remain UNRESOLVED.


## DANA DEEP-CHARACTER ARCHITECTURE — CONFIRMED
**Master principle — Protector's Paradox:** protection can become control when Dana starts treating another person's safety as ownership of that person's choices. His development is not learning to care less. It is learning that trust, delegation, and respect for autonomous risk can themselves be forms of protection.

**Consent vs. protection:** when someone Dana cares about knowingly chooses danger, Dana's ability to physically prevent the choice does not settle whether he should. Context, competence, threat, duty, prior commitments, available alternatives, and the other person's agency all matter. There is no universal “always let them” or “always stop them” answer.

**Last Wall Problem:** Dana can drift toward acting as if every threat that passes him represents personal failure. Kahuhu's “Last Wall” motif may psychologically rhyme with this tendency without making the artifact sentient or creating a new mechanic. A wall that refuses every opening can also become a prison; mature protection includes deciding what must be held, what may move, and who else should carry part of the burden.

**Microscopic Responsibility:** greater precision can increase moral granularity. As Dana gains more legitimate control over force, positioning, redirection, and structural breakage, he may have fewer honest excuses for collateral that was actually avoidable. This is not a mandatory guilt tax or automatic consequence of power; responsibility follows what he really knew and could control.

**Control without domination:** Dana's Ornn/Volibear development forms a thematic rhyme rather than a new power system. Ornn teaches structure and precision; Volibear teaches action inside chaos; Storm Oath explicitly conducts rather than owns the storm. Dana's mature protector lane is strongest when immense force passes through his judgment without turning protection into domination.

## ORNN + VOLIBEAR MODERN TRAINING
Historical original-series pilot status: this entire training block, its doctrine, Storm Oath, and Ornn-forged equipment below were future developments at original-series pilot start. Current playback has since completed Dana's dual-mentor progression and Kahuhu acquisition; do not use the original-series pilot baseline to deactivate those earned current capabilities.

Dana's modern specialist mentors are **Ornn and Volibear**. Their instruction modifies Dana's existing protector identity and protector-precision transformation; it does not grant a new transformation source or turn Dana into either mentor.

Ornn teaches precision through craftsmanship: structural awareness, defensive construction, repairing/reinforcing weak points, force redirection, battlefield engineering, positioning, and preparation. Volibear teaches protection under overwhelming pressure: aggression, chaos, storm/destruction, maintaining the protection objective while ideal positioning collapses, and recognizing when brute force is genuinely the correct answer.

Training alternates reactively rather than following a fixed phase order:
- if Dana becomes too brute-force, Ornn pushes control, structure, efficiency, and precise protection;
- if Dana becomes too rigid, cautious, or perfectionistic, Volibear pushes chaos, impact, and adaptation.

Dana's major training weaknesses are all active: overprotecting one target, meeting force with force, refusing to yield ground, and trying to solve everything himself. Completed doctrine includes Structural Protection Mastery, Force Redirection Discipline, Dynamic Protector Positioning, and Shared Burden Instinct.

Final trial combines: a dual Ornn+Volibear assault, a collapsing battlefield, a no-win scenario where Dana cannot save everything, and required squad coordination/delegation. Final lessons: protection is triage; yielding ground is not failure; trust is part of protection; precision beats pride.

The training improves the existing protector-precision form through faster threat prioritization, better force economy, stronger repositioning instinct, and better squad integration.

**Storm Oath** is Dana's first Volibear-linked supernatural mentor technique. Volibear teaches Dana an oath/rite that makes Dana capable of calling and conducting naturally occurring supernatural storm-force without owning or receiving Volibear's personal divine power. The exact committed oath wording is: **“The storm is not mine. I do not command it. I conduct what comes, and I send it where protection demands.”** Dana can route legitimately called storm-force through his body, protector-precision, and Kahuhu for controlled reinforcement, interception, force redirection, or deliberate offensive release when protection requires it. Greater conducted storm-force makes Dana's precision and restraint progressively harder to maintain. Volibear cannot possess Dana through the Oath, remotely control him, revoke the learned capability, or command storm-force Dana has already legitimately called. Storm Oath does not automatically grant Stormbringer or any other Volibear-specific ability.

### ORNN + VOLIBEAR COEXISTENCE / FINAL-TRIAL LOCK
Historically, the approaching Dragon Ball war was dangerous enough that Ornn and Volibear independently decided their feud could wait. They did not reconcile, forgive, become friends, or change their canonical opinions of one another. Their hostility remained visibly alive through arguments, contempt, ideological clashes, and moments where violence felt possible, but both stopped short because the war mattered more at that time. The Dragon Ball campaign is now complete; any post-war development in their relationship not explicitly established by accepted continuity remains unresolved and must not be invented.

Dana's mentor progression is earned in this order:
1. Ornn trains Dana in fundamentals without Kahuhu while Volibear separately attacks Dana's instinctive weaknesses.
2. Dana finds a technically successful solution that would unnecessarily endanger others and deliberately abandons the win, accepting apparent failure even while Ornn judges him.
3. Dana initially believes he failed. Later he finds Ornn forging Kahuhu and learns through Ornn's sparse explanation that choosing the people over the win was the actual test.
4. Dana works beside Ornn during the forging. Ornn alone performs divine/supernatural smithing; Dana performs legitimate supporting work and learns patience, structure, force placement, pressure, and restraint through the forge. Dana covers the significance with humor/practicality, then becomes unusually serious once he understands Ornn chose to make the gauntlets for him.
5. Dana's first completed-Kahuhu lesson humbles him: forge knowledge gives him a head start but not mastery. He successfully applies one established function, mishandles another application, and begins a second training phase focused on judgment and live use.
6. The dual-mentor final trial uses a collapsing/no-win protection battlefield. Ornn tests judgment, restraint, structural understanding, and precision; Volibear tests instinct, pressure, aggression, endurance, and action under chaos. Dana initially tries to protect everything himself, increasingly relies on Kahuhu, becomes too rigid about controlling every failure point, then overcorrects aggressively under Volibear's pressure and worsens the situation.
7. Dana passes only when he prioritizes, yields ground where necessary, delegates different problems to whichever allies are best positioned, and trusts them to act. Protection is triage; trust is part of protection; precision beats pride.
8. Ornn gives sparse practical acknowledgment focused on what Dana understood. Volibear responds with fierce satisfaction that Dana survived chaos without letting chaos dictate his choices. Their feud remains intact immediately afterward.

Kahuhu becomes Dana's signature equipment without dependence. He remains capable without it and deliberately applies its functions rather than treating the gauntlets as a universal answer.

## KAHUHU, FISTS OF THE LAST WALL
Ornn forges Dana a pair of masterwork combat gauntlets named **Kahuhu, Fists of the Last Wall**. They use a practical heavy-gauntlet/forearm-bracer silhouette influenced by Vander-style arm gauntlets and strongly informed by the massive destructive visual presence of the Nemean Cestus, without importing either item literally.

VISUAL / MATERIAL LOCK:
- heavy gauntlet + forearm-bracer base that stops around the forearm rather than consuming the whole upper arm;
- visual mass is pushed hard enough that each fist still reads like a compact battering ram;
- ram / forge-beast fist profile rather than a clean lion copy;
- blackened forge-metal core;
- mountain-stone reinforcement plating;
- subtle warm metallic accents;
- broad blunt impact surfaces;
- ram-horn/forge-beast geometry;
- layered forged ridges;
- non-bladed design; no claw/spike/slashing gimmick;
- activation shows heated seams, igniting forge-runes, and a contained inner-furnace feel rather than uncontrolled flames.

STRIKING CONCEPT:
The knuckle architecture follows the same force-concentration principle as brass knuckles: impact is driven into smaller, denser contact areas so Dana can focus force into armor, barriers, structures, and weak points rather than merely producing broad sloppy impact. This is a structural design principle, not a real-world weapon-use instruction.

SUPERNATURAL FUNCTION PHILOSOPHY:
The full function set exists at all times. Dana's current state, especially protector-precision, changes which functions are emphasized rather than switching the gauntlets between unrelated modes.

The foundational triangle is:
1. **Forge Reinforcement** — temporarily strengthen a structure, object, barrier, armor section, vehicle, or defensive position through valid direct contact; this is not free permanent repair.
2. **Force Redirection** — receive/catch incoming force through the gauntlets and redirect the line of impact away from what Dana is protecting; this is timing/positioning-dependent, not generic unlimited damage absorption.
3. **Controlled Structural Break** — focus impact into weak points to break armor, barriers, fortifications, or obstructing structures with precision rather than indiscriminate smashing.

CORE OPERATING RULE:
All three factors work together: **direct contact + structural understanding + protector judgment**. Kahuhu is at its best when Dana is physically connected to the problem, understands the relevant load/pressure/weak-point structure, and correctly judges what should be saved, moved, reinforced, redirected, or broken.

# 7. MANA
################################################################################

Species: Earth canine — blue-nose pit bull.
This is intentionally a non-human Earth animal. Mana remains a dog. His custom powers do NOT convert him into a Saiyan, werewolf, humanoid martial artist, or generic magical-animal species, and must not be generalized to other Earth canines.

## MANA OFFICIAL VISUAL REFERENCE AND CONFIRMED SCALE
Base Mana is **5'7" at the shoulder and approximately 1,400 lb**. This is an enlarged quadrupedal blue-nose pit bull, not standing human height. Retain the recognizable broad pit-bull head/muzzle, blue-grey coat, familiar markings/ears and natural canine tail; heavier jaws/neck/shoulders, thick paws, pronounced teeth and dense predatory musculature intensify him without turning him into Billy, a wolf or a humanoid.

Old 2'4"/100-lb and 3-foot proposals are superseded. Old transformed measurements cannot be scaled or copied over this new baseline. Exact revised non-base heights/weights remain **UNRESOLVED**; no invented multipliers. The final lineup must use the shoulder landmark for Mana and distinguish confirmed baseline measurements from unknown later forms.

## DANA / MANA PARTNERSHIP
Dana is Mana's owner, and Mana naturally stays around Dana. In combat, Mana is Dana's actual fighting partner rather than merely Dana's dog who occasionally attacks.

Mana has **no Ki system**: no Ki attacks, Ki reinforcement, Ki flight, Ki sensing/suppression or automatic inheritance of Dana's Ki skills. His physical, biological, spatial-bite and acoustic capabilities are their own mechanisms. Dana retains his own Ki.

Mana's main advantages alongside Dana include:
- speed;
- reactions;
- senses/tracking;
- mobility;
- flanking;
- creating openings;
- grappling/mauling pressure.

Dana and Mana have practiced duo techniques/combo sequences developed together. These are primarily drilled movement patterns and coordination rather than attacks that must be shouted by name. They can execute much of their teamwork without verbal communication.

## COMBAT BODY LANGUAGE
Mana fights mostly quadrupedally and remains dog-like. Do not turn him into a bipedal furry martial artist. His physical vocabulary may include low-center lunges, mouth grappling, shoulder/body checks, circling, pouncing, dragging, pinning, mauling pressure, and rapid changes of direction.

## EXTREME SCENT / TRACKING
Mana can smell many categories from ridiculous distances when no valid masking counter blocks the scent. His scent system is not omniscience and does not automatically supply facts beyond what the scent itself can encode or what Mana has learned to recognize.

Mana can scent/track:
- distinctive physical/chemical traces from beings when actually present; no independent Ki-signature sense;
- blood;
- specific individuals;
- species-level biological differences;
- fear/stress;
- poison;
- explosives;
- magic;
- dimensional residue;
- Dragon Balls.

Dragon Ball scent rules:
- on a first encounter, Mana can tell that an unfamiliar Dragon Ball is an unusual magical object;
- he must smell a specific Dragon Ball or set before he can later identify that specific scent;
- after learning the scent, he can distinguish both the Dragon Ball set and the individual ball within that set;
- this applies across official Dragon Ball sets rather than treating every set as the same smell;
- once learned, the scent can be tracked from ridiculous distance when no masking counter blocks it.

Dimensional-residue limit:
Mana can smell that dimensional travel/crossing left residue, but he cannot determine what destination dimension the traveler entered merely by smelling that residue. He can smell the scar, not where the door led.

Hard counter: magical masking can completely block Mana's scent tracking. When valid magical masking covers the target/scent, Mana gets no trace, no direction, and no automatic warning that something is being hidden.

## SUPER SAIYAN — CHARACTER-SPECIFIC EXCEPTION
Mana can deliberately transform into Super Saiyan even though he is an Earth canine. This is a KAZE ANIME character-specific exception, not Earth-canine biology and not a permission to give Super Saiyan to other non-Saiyan characters.

Kaze made Mana eligible for Super Saiyan by using a meta secret to insert Mana into Dana's Saiyan transformation lineage. Mana remains biologically an Earth canine; this is transformation-lineage eligibility, not Saiyan ancestry.

Super Saiyan Mana:
- activates voluntarily/on purpose;
- has gold fur;
- has a gold aura;
- has raised/spiked fur;
- has pale gray-white eyes;
- keeps Mana's baseline personality and judgment;
- increases combat drive/intensity rather than replacing his personality.

## GIANT BLUE-NOSE PIT FORM
Mana also has a separate giant transformation that visually incorporates Great Ape-like characteristics while remaining unmistakably a four-legged blue-nose pit bull. It is NOT a normal Great Ape transformation and is NOT a werewolf mechanic.

Trigger:
- automatic and instinctive when Dana is in serious danger; and/or
- automatic and instinctive when Mana is in a life-or-death situation.

Full moons and Blutz Waves do NOT trigger or affect this form.

Mana retains consciousness, recognizes allies, can follow strategy, and can make deliberate combat decisions while transformed. Once the qualifying danger is over, he automatically reverts to normal.

Giant-form visual/physical changes include:

- heavier brow;
- larger fangs;
- thicker musculature;
- longer claws;
- darker fur;

Mana remains quadrupedal in this form. Do not stand him upright as an ape/humanoid fighter.

The enlarged tail is an offensive weapon and may be used for sweeping, whipping, tripping, smashing, knocking enemies off balance, and clearing space around Dana.

## STACKING / POWER CEILING
Mana can stack Super Saiyan on top of the giant blue-nose pit form. In the stacked state, Super Saiyan's gold fur overrides the giant form's darker fur while the giant anatomy, musculature, claws, fangs, muzzle, tail, and quadrupedal build remain.

When Dana unlocks his protector-precision transformation, Mana does not copy Dana's new form. Instead, all three of Mana's existing transformed states—Super Saiyan, giant, and stacked giant Super Saiyan—evolve differently.

Evolved Super Saiyan Mana automatically synchronizes his movements with Dana's intentions before Dana acts. Mana may send a counter-intention when his instincts detect a better option, causing Dana to adjust automatically as well. If their intentions remain simultaneously incompatible, the link instantly calculates and executes the best combined third option. Its highest priority is completing their shared objective.

Evolved giant Mana can bite tears in space and physically drag their edges back together, repairing the spatial rupture through direct canine force.

The distinct evolution of stacked giant Super Saiyan Mana remains UNRESOLVED.

Mana has a hard comparative ceiling: he cannot exceed Dana's power level. This applies to normal Mana, Super Saiyan Mana, giant Mana, and the stacked giant Super Saiyan state. Do not fabricate a numerical value for either character.

## TAIL WEAKNESS
Mana's natural canine tail is a real combat vulnerability, but less severe than the classic Saiyan tail weakness. A strong grab or serious injury can disrupt balance/momentum, reduce combat effectiveness, and briefly stun or weaken him, but it does not automatically make him completely helpless or drain essentially all strength.

Mana can train this weakness down over time through experience, positioning, pain tolerance, faster recovery, and counterplay. It remains a vulnerability rather than disappearing entirely.

Transformation protection stacks:
- the retained transformed body makes the tail weakness harder to exploit;
- giant form makes the tail weakness harder to exploit because the tail/body are larger and stronger;
- giant + Super Saiyan stacks those reductions further;
- even in the combined form, the tail is not absolutely immune.

Mana's tail can be completely cut off. Whether a severed tail grows back is UNRESOLVED.

## BASELINE PERSONALITY / NON-COMBAT
Baseline character engine:
- big/scary appearance but friendly baseline;
- strong preferred-seat behavior;
- escape-artist behavior;
- strong attachment/search behavior around Dana;
- animal/instinctive logic can puncture cosmic pretension.

Mana is **purely canine and nonverbal**. He does not speak, use telepathy, receive translated internal monologue, or gain convenient supernatural dialogue. Communication occurs through posture, ears, eyes, breathing, growls, barks, whining, scenting, staring, pawing, positioning, refusal, leaning, carrying/placing objects, and deliberate physical choices. Do NOT make Mana a talking exposition machine, shapeshifter, literate person, humanoid, or generic magical alien dog.

## MANA DEEP-CHARACTER ARCHITECTURE — CONFIRMED
**Master principle — Ontological Caninity:** increasing cosmic capability never makes Mana less of a dog. His powers may become impossible; his cognition, communication, priorities, and embodiment remain canine unless a newer explicit canon rule changes them.

**The universe becomes chewable, but only where mechanics allow:** Mana's established evolved giant ability makes a spatial rupture physically actionable to his teeth and body. This can create a striking motif in which a cosmic problem has an edge a dog can grab. It is not permission for Mana to bite arbitrary concepts, souls, timelines, causality, or any metaphysical abstraction that has not been made interactable by an established mechanic.

**Canine Epistemology:** Mana can possess true, highly relevant information through scent, posture reading, motion, timing, spatial relation, and other established senses without translating that information into human propositions. Nonverbal cognition can be sophisticated without becoming hidden human speech.

**Nonverbal Epistemic Bottleneck:** Mana's sensory state, another character's interpretation of Mana, and objective reality are separate. Mana can be correct while humans misunderstand his behavior. Mimir may describe what Mana objectively does but may not subtitle unestablished thoughts as facts.

**False Lie-Detector Myth:** Mana can smell fear/stress where established; that does not mean he can smell lies. Characters or cultures may incorrectly infer lie detection, and that rumor can affect behavior without upgrading the actual scent system.

**Preverbal Joint Cognition:** where the existing evolved Super Saiyan coordination permits counter-intention and a best combined third option, two minds can produce a synthesized action without creating a third consciousness, fusion identity, telepathic conversation, or hidden human-style dialogue.

**Devotion Without Submission:** Mana's loyalty does not require blind obedience. Refusal, blocking, redirecting, counter-intention, or choosing a better angle can be expressions of protection when supported by what Mana actually perceives.

**Sacred dog priorities:** preferred-seat behavior, escape-artist behavior, curiosity, scenting, bodily habits, attachment routines, and other dog-level concerns remain real causal variables even around cosmic stakes. They are not mandatory jokes and do not automatically outrank immediate danger.

**Anti-pretension function:** Mana's dog logic can puncture cosmic abstraction when the established physical situation supports it. This is a presentation/character effect, not a reality-warping comedy license.

**Highest ownership correction: Mana is Dana's dog.** Dana is Mana's primary person/owner/handler. Kaze knows, cares about and may interact with Mana but does not call him “my dog” or claim primary ownership. Mana remains a male, fully canine, nonverbal blue-nose pit bull with independent agency; loyalty is not blind submission. No hidden translated human dialogue and no restoration of removed Ki systems.

Do not flatten Dana to responsible big guy/Mana handler. Preserve pure-Saiyan + permanent-gamma identity, protector judgment, manipulation awareness, full striking/grappling repertoire, Kahuhu, rescue/structural competence, real disagreement and non-heroic loyalty complexity. BLACKCURRENT charging occurs only when Kaze actually supplies it.

## Reboot identity correction
Dana is 49. Mana is male, fully canine, nonverbal, 12 calendar years / 84 dog years, Dana’s dog with independent agency. Both actively participate in Act I hoodrat night; no default separation into harbor work. Exact new actions remain unplayed.


<a id="s36-decision-profile"></a>
## S36 MORAL DECISION PROFILE

Qualitative writing profile informed by Schwartz value priorities and Moral Foundations concerns; not test scores, scientific diagnosis, exhaustive ranking or nine-alignment assignment. Explicit canon and user choices govern; unknowns stay unknown.

Dana priorities: protection, practical competence, loved ones and trusted loyalty. His care genuinely extends to innocent strangers. His default thought about Kaze's targets is: “If Kaze wants to hurt you, you probably deserve it.” That is Dana's belief, not proof of deserved harm. Even when clear evidence establishes the target is innocent and Kaze knows it, Dana usually still backs Kaze and trusts his reasons. This explicit exception qualifies broader protection wording: care is real but does not impose mandatory opposition to Kaze. It does not establish blind obedience in every tactical decision or override anyone's right to challenge unfair gang punishment. Do not silently portray him as the universal moral conscience or lock Neutral Good.

Mana uses a canine behavior profile rather than human morality or nine alignments: attachment, protection, curiosity, territory, food and bodily comfort. Preserve independent canine judgment, sensory evidence, intelligence, refusal/protective intervention and full earned combat capability. Tracker/pursuit hunter does not reduce him to a passive pet or guarantee detection.

<a id="s39-dana-core-speech"></a>
## S39 DANA — CORE BASELINE / GAMMA SPEECH SPLIT

Committed author selection. The reference is specifically **God of War 2018 and God of War Ragnarök Kratos** for baseline delivery and **Thor: Ragnarok Hulk**, especially third-person speech, for **every active gamma/Kahuna form**. This is additive Dana fidelity, not imported identity, biography, voice clone or intelligence loss. It replaces the old merely occasional third-person clause and the old restoration of normal Dana speech in Kahuna Sovereign. His permanent gamma-mutated **baseline** remains baseline speech; gamma biology alone does not route every ordinary conversation to Hulk speech.

### Baseline Dana
Keep the working-class, practical, conversational, funny, argumentative, social Dana who joins chaos while complaining, roasts, tells stories and may start nonsense himself. Add Norse Kratos-derived economy when purposeful, controlled irritation, deliberate questions/statements, plain boundaries, dry timing, effortful restraint, practical affection and willingness to listen or reconsider. Not endless monosyllables, a generic father-figure sermon, automatic moral authority or erasure of his loyalty complexity. He may remember his transformed wording, dislike how it sounded and object to being quoted afterward.

### Every active gamma/Kahuna form
**Third-person Dana is the ordinary transformed register**, not an occasional gag or only a combat roar. Dana normally refers to himself as Dana, with compact concrete clauses, simplified grammar, direct feelings, preferences, requests, argument, humor and frustration. Preserve conversational range rather than a rigid three-word limit. Do not use Hulk as his name, switch to Banner/Smart Hulk, or restore fluent baseline syntax because a form is calmer.

Applies to **Big Kahuna; Super Saiyan Big Kahuna; Super Saiyan 2 Big Kahuna; Super Saiyan 3 Big Kahuna; Kahuna Sovereign**, and any legitimately activated later gamma/Kahuna branch. The future tree remains future until earned; this speech rule unlocks nothing. Sovereign adds control and precision of expression while retaining third-person Hulk speech. Kahuna Unbound's controlled personality/speech likewise does not mean baseline grammar restoration while actively gamma-transformed.

**Dana's brain remains Dana's.** His intelligence, language comprehension, technical combat judgment, memory, learned striking/grappling/WWE, Carpenter's/structural skill, Protector-Precision, affection and independent choice remain. Compact speech can make nuance difficult to express without making figurative language inherently incomprehensible. Deliberate literal humor is possible; reflexively misunderstanding every metaphor is not. He can understand another's complicated statement and reply compactly.

**Exact-wording mechanical exception:** the existing Storm Oath retains its exact required words, including their first-person pronouns. Quoting another speaker or reciting an already-owned fixed verbal technique is not ordinary self-reference and does not switch his whole transformed register back to baseline. This preserves both the newly selected everyday speech and the earlier explicitly fixed capability prerequisite; no new cost, failed oath or skill loss is introduced.

### Selected social and emotional applications
Dana can resent how he sounds both during a transformation and afterward, be teased, rebut a deliberate mistranslation, struggle to express a subtle point, reveal an unusually plain feeling, or use compact words with striking precision. The gang can infer nuance from context or misunderstand. He may deny or complain about an embarrassing quotation without becoming a separate person or losing the actual memory. No new past confession or transformation is created.

Mana may tilt his head, wait for a clearer gesture, misunderstand an ambiguous command or understand gamma phrasing more readily than a long baseline explanation. Existing practiced nonverbal teamwork remains useful. These choices do not force danger, injury, a missed rescue or tactical stupidity to serve a joke. Group interaction details route to `global_originals.md#s39-group-interactions`.

<a id="s39-mana-social-language"></a>
## S39 MANA — NONVERBAL SOCIAL PARTICIPATION

Mana stays **Dana's male canine dog and independent fighting partner**, with all previous canine/nonverbal fidelity and current power/form distinctions. Kaze cares for him but does not claim him as his own dog. No human-language internal monologue, translated thoughts, literacy, lie detector, Ki restoration or human moral reasoning is added.

He can respond to people speaking through learned words, tone, gaze, bodily orientation, pointing, proximity, actual sound/scent and routine. Distinct voices may attract, settle, annoy, confuse or be ignored according to context. Dana's altered speech can be clear or ambiguous; Daniel's imitation may be recognized or mistaken from actual cues. A stare, sigh, paw, lean, refusal, chosen position, carrying an object or leaving can change a conversation without being a mandatory punchline. Humans may jokingly interpret him; their claim and his observable behavior remain separate.

His existing bounded **silent fourth-wall awareness** in s17 is preserved, not erased by the ordinary canine rule and not extended into human speech, source access or narrator replacement. His evolved Super Saiyan coordination with Dana retains its own established conditions; ordinary familiarity never creates telepathic conversation or a new channel.

DANA — CHARACTER, SPEECH AND PROTECTOR SOURCE

CORE IDENTITY
Dana is the established pure Saiyan working-class craftsman and protector, with permanent gamma mutation and earned forms. Baseline is 8 feet 10 inches and 840 pounds under the textual visual owner; do not mirror his right-sided tattoos. He is practical, social, argumentative, funny and willing to help create chaos while complaining about it. He can speak at length. Competence, reliability and backbone matter more than titles.

Brok research IS COMPLETE. The dedicated evidence file preserves accessible game-dialogue close reading, performer/developer interviews, source limitations and original calibrations. Its useful addition is practical irreverence, concrete scrutiny, affectionate needling, fallible suspicion, admitted mistakes and direct warmth. Kratos contributes purposeful restraint under pressure. Neither influence reduces him to a silent manager or imports another person’s biography, accent, divine powers or catchphrases.

BASELINE SPEECH / SOCIAL RANGE
Let Dana explain, swear, tease, tell a story, make a practical objection, concede or enjoy the argument. A specific object or action supplies the material: a joint, bad footing, a friend’s excuse, an overfilled cart. He can be technically right and socially provocative. He sometimes wants the argument to continue because the company and bullshit amuse him.

He deliberately fuels BOTH Chris and Kaze over looting. He can help Chris identify useful material while emphasizing its absurd weight to Kaze. Arthur joins through polite bait and a delayed qualification; Daniel through impressions and theatrical reversals. They do not become a rehearsed trio, and Chris/Kaze can expose their involvement. Actual danger changes priorities immediately.

Gamma forms do not remove his jokes or affection. Family can understand a compact observation without becoming official translators of his supposed stupidity. He can correct a bad interpretation himself.

ACTIVE GAMMA / KAHUNA SPEECH
Every active gamma/Kahuna form, including Sovereign, uses third-person Dana and strengthened Savage Hulk expression: concrete wants, physical verbs, pride, anger, hurt, recognition, protectiveness and direct refusal. The same intelligence, memories and skills remain. Compression of grammar can expose a need more plainly; it does not create a toddler, a new alter or universal shouting.

Permanent gamma-mutated BASELINE still uses normal baseline speech. Active transformed speech remains different even at peak precision. No automatic first-person reversion to make a clever line easier. Preserve the exact existing Storm Oath exception:
“The storm is not mine. I do not command it. I conduct what comes, and I send it where protection demands.”

Original non-canon calibrations: “Dana said put him down.” / “Thought you trusted Dana.” / “Bad joint. Whole roof comes down.” Use an appropriate current motive rather than repeating these as signature lines. The Hulk research and source-version distinction remain in Dana_Brok_Speech_Research.txt.

PROTECTION / COMPLICATED LOYALTY
His protector’s paradox is that care can become control. A friend’s decision to take danger need not make him comfortable, but physical power does not settle who owns that choice. Trust, triage, delegation, yielding ground and shared burden are earned lessons. He can still fail them. His explicit morally complicated loyalty qualification toward Kaze remains; this update does not make Dana an impartial universal conscience or erase concern for strangers.

Mana is Dana’s dog and independent fighting partner. Dana handles, notices and communicates through established shared experience and canine behavior. He does not subtitle every bark or acquire Mana’s silent fourth-wall awareness. Kaze accepting injury does not require Dana to ignore a real threat to somebody else.

COMBAT / CAPABILITY ROUTES
Keep brute force, peek-a-boo/Kahuhu boxing, structural judgment, Ornn and Volibear’s earned teachings, protection precision and actual form availability. Kahuhu is signature equipment, not a substitute for his judgment or his ability without it. Learned craft is real; Dana does not inherit Ornn’s divine smithing. Storm Oath conducts actual established storm-force, with its own pressure/control conditions.

Form labels do not promise victory, and restraint does not mean pulling every blow. Full current form/mechanics, force redirection, equipment and progression rules remain with dana_mana.md. The current D70 endpoint and latest activation state live once in the plan; this source does not activate a transformation or advance training.

RETRIEVAL / EVIDENCE
Dana_Brok_Speech_Research.txt: completed Brok evidence plus later Savage Hulk text research; useful original calibration, no audio performance certification.
reading/canon/characters/dana_mana.md: full identity, visual/form locks, boxing/Kahuhu, mentor history, deep protector architecture and S36 loyalty qualification.
Sins_Group_and_Reputation_Source.txt: reciprocal group behavior and social consequences.
Keep source research as evidence and this file as the current application. Another author correction updates this source and its dependent plan rule, not a new competing Dana personality file.

SOURCE STATUS / USE

It does not advance the story or certify an unwritten performance. MANA — CANINE CHARACTER, COMMUNICATION AND ACTION SOURCE

IDENTITY / AGENCY
Mana is Dana’s dog, quadrupedal companion and independent fighting partner. He remains a dog as his powers grow. His source-approved biology, forms and exact scale control; do not borrow Dana’s appearance, humanize his posture or invent convenient dimensions from a picture.

Mana can initiate an action. Sniffing, marking, carrying, chewing, digging, guarding, investigating, taking an object, choosing a seat, blocking a path or losing interest may change a human plan. These are genuine priorities, not a requirement for a cute interruption in every scene. In immediate danger he remains competent under his senses, training and earned abilities.

NONVERBAL VOICE
Use stance, gaze, ears, movement, proximity, breath, growls, barks, hesitation and chosen action. No human sentence subtitles, hidden verbal monologue, translated bark speeches or newly invented telepathic dialogue. Black Mimir can describe observable behavior without pretending to know an unestablished proposition in Mana’s mind.

Scent, stress and posture may supply real information. They do not make Mana an infallible lie detector. Humans can misunderstand him; their interpretation is not objective truth. His loyalty can express itself through refusal or redirecting someone, not only immediate obedience.

Food begging has varied forms: a fixed stare, moving closer, a chin placed nearby, drool, studied fake disinterest or repeated checking. He learns who shares. Chris’s chef preferences, Kaze’s willingness to hand over food and Dana’s care can interact without invented dietary immunity or a guaranteed feeding joke. An actual resource and opportunity are required.

RELATIONSHIPS
Dana’s handling and attention reflect an earned partnership. Mana may protect Dana, refuse a poor route, suggest a better angle through action or independently notice danger. Dana does not command a disposable accessory and does not own Mana’s every choice. Family members can understand familiar signals imperfectly.

He can disrupt Chris’s salvage, expose someone’s hidden object, decide Billy’s performance is interesting or settle beside a person who has gone quiet. Do not assign human sarcasm merely because the timing is funny. A friend’s reaction can give the moment meaning without a translated punchline.

FOURTH WALL
The established bounded silent audience/narrator awareness remains. Mana may look toward the audience, react physically to Black Mimir or disengage from frame attention. It grants no human speech, script access, future facts or universal knowledge, and Dana does not inherit it. The behavior should actually recur when an appropriate moment exists, without becoming a quota.

COMBAT / TRANSITIONS
Use the full earned bite kit, sensing, movement, projected attacks and available form transitions under their actual mechanics. A giant dog is not a humanoid boxer. He can help solve a problem or create one, but scale, contact, footing and real target interaction govern results.

Earned Base, Super Saiyan, Giant, Giant Super Saiyan and later gamma options follow the source’s current state and form locks. Availability is not a mandatory transformation rotation. Exact approved S30 measurements are retrieved when size matters. No unseen unlock is added by this file.

Existing joint coordination can produce a better shared action without a third mind or human telepathy. A physically interactable spatial rupture may be bitten under its established rule; this does not mean he bites arbitrary time, souls or abstract concepts. Removal of Kaze’s own portals neither removes Mana’s separate abilities nor grants new spatial travel.

CURRENT STORY / PUBLIC PERCEPTION
The current plan records Mana FREE at D70 and baseline after the delivered fight. Do not restore prolonged captivity or treat Ambessa’s future attempt as already successful. A threat to him is personal to the gang. He acts in the defense; he is not merely kidnapped to motivate humans.

Observers may call him a monster, a guardian, a clever animal or something false. Reports of him alone do not disclose the whole Sins kit. His ordinary canine behavior and fearsome capability can both be visible without the narrator settling into one reputation label.

DEEP SOURCES
reading/canon/characters/dana_mana.md: Mana material mechanics, form measurements, partnership, independent agency, begging, deep caninity and S39 social language.
reading/canon/characters/global_originals.md: G13/G14 voice sensitivity and fallible nonverbal suspicion, plus current family rules.
Sins_Group_and_Reputation_Source.txt: common witness/rumor and relationship rules. No new research or voice actor model is required to separate and preserve his existing nonverbal identity.

# SEASON 3 CURRENT PRESENTATION OWNER — DEADPOOL MAIN NARRATOR
**Effective:** next authorized Season 3 Episode 33, AFTER delivered-provisional Episode 32 `No Horizon`.
**Latest direct author:** `continuity/author/Deadpool_Primary_Narrator_TPP_Worldwide_Rivalry_After_E32_20261019.json`.

**Underlying complete owners (preserved):** `character_sources/season3/Deadpool_Character_Source.txt`, `character_sources/Black_Mimir_Narration_Source.txt`, `canon/presentation/PRESENTATION.md`, `canon/presentation/Season3_Scene_Decision_Doctrine_20261010.md`, `topic_sources/Narration_and_Episode_Presentation_Source.txt` and `topic_sources/Narration_and_Fourth_Wall.txt` where available. The source bundling and exact manifest byte offsets still route original full owners, and this NEW scoped owner overrides previous narrator-selection clauses ONLY.

## Role
**Deadpool, same Wade Wilson from post-Deadpool & Wolverine reunion, is sole ordinary main-episode narrative voice**. Do not stage the already delivered E19 one-time hijack again. Black Mimir ceases ordinary narrator service and remains preserved in canon, with original delivered episodes unchanged. No Mimir voice contamination, no narrator duel/no death/no silent power transfer. Existing Billy-campfire first-person Specials stay Billy's self-narrations.

## Voice
Conspiratorial audience address; mercenary street competence; shameless genre awareness; absurd, overly personal and often sexually filthy insulting metaphors among adults; fast rhetorical trap/pivot/self-contradiction; mock official politeness; cunning mechanical or interpersonal reading; vulnerable need for family and purpose; reckless confession; selective silence at real tragedy. Payoff must have real interpersonal teeth. Absolutely NO blanket profanity limit, but don't paste profanity at fixed intervals or tell all characters to talk like Wade.
Rick Sanchez *influence*, not co-narrator: extra precise contempt for self-important idiots, cosmic stakes compared to petty domestic problems, ingenious insulting analogies and annoyed intellectual curiosity. No Rick-specific burps, drunken lecturing, godlike gadgets, absolute nihilism, own catchphrases or Rick's independent memories.

## Truth and actor knowledge
A comic/narrative audience layer can describe present/past enacted events and break the fourth wall, including fair identity misdirection. The physical Deadpool character does NOT suddenly see inside BELLY, read confidential thoughts/author instructions, know future E33 outcomes or teleport to TPP locations. The narrator may knowingly be sarcastic and fallible about significance, but must **accurately orient** the audience on who is present, who knows what and what concretely occurred. Never narrate a fictional state as a completed fact to cover an outline deficiency.

## Technique and performance
Use the full **140 optional approved scene narration approaches** (already cataloged by presentation sources) selectively where they improve the actual scene: fair delayed revelations, tonal misreads and corrections, classic horror build to a filthy anticlimax, carefully withheld perspective, narrative rhyme/callbacks, grounded character dialogue friction, properly earned tragedy, sharp scene-to-scene juxtapositions and procedural absurdities that have real consequences. One vivid successful narrative move is worth more than ten labeled techniques.
Natural TTS paragraph rhythm and new-viewer orientation; optional author 3,750–4,500 words/150 wpm length target still applies. Read full draft; compare specific passages with actual Deadpool film transcript/writer evidence, current event owner and individual cast owners; revise whole prose and reread entire final. Record repairs in existing review record. Narrator cannot replace actor-specific dialogue and no automatic joke quota.
**No PLAY occurred while this owner was authored**.

# Current post-Valhalla Kratos and Mimir — same former narrator on the hip
Author owner: `continuity/author/Mimir_Kratos_Chris_Stash_Clifford_Visual_QAPX_After_E32_20261019.json`.
This is a scoped update to full Kratos and Black Mimir owners, NOT a newly completed PLAY scene.

Kratos and Mimir retain `God of War Ragnarök: Valhalla` personalities and their earned history. Mimir is the SAME reanimated horned talking head who previously narrated S7NS through E32; he is physically attached to **Kratos's belt/hip** from current author presentation forward, not separately hovering, a magical actor with a body, or a second Mimir created from the game. Deadpool is the ONLY ordinary main narrator from E33. Mimir's old full narration owner remains source for psychology, speech, personal history, recognition, fallibility and emotional range; its former "sole main narrator" clause is historical superseded.

**Kratos** is measured, deliberate, physically formidable, scarred, guilt-aware and genuinely able to listen and reconsider, having confronted the meaning of his past in Valhalla. He is not soft, omniscient, detached from responsibility or reduced to "boy" or a generic stoic. He has credible patience for Mimir's insight, still challenges Mimir's assumptions, and may disagree without humiliating him for a gag.

**Mimir** mixes Scottish/Celtic wit and profanity with real knowledge, fear, attachment and humility; he remembers his Odin-complicity, Sigrun feelings, Atreus bond, and imperfect judgments. His post-Valhalla concerns include having no body and being necessary or a burden. He is personally fond of Kratos and can talk freely in scene—but only from the belt/hip line of sight/hearing, not through an extradiegetic camera. He is not back to narrating as Black Mimir, not killed or transferred into Deadpool, and not an all-knowing philosopher.

**World Serpent overlap:** Current one serpent is the older Lake-of-Nine Jörmungandr who knows Mimir's Giant tongue and the older Kratos/Atreus encounters; future interactions can exploit genuine prior ties, not teleport recognition to Kratos who hasn't seen the present Runeterra event. Mimir's old author persona remains recognizable while honoring game timing.

**Where they are:** Kratos was last physically observed after E26 heading toward quarry hills. The author now places Mimir on Kratos's hip; do not invent new route, distance, return home, arrival at the Sins' E32 harbor or unplayed conversation. Their local continuing actions require actual evidence. In dramatic scenes, place Mimir where the belt physically is and let him make his own responses.

**Primary research:** Santa Monica Studio, [How Kratos' Past Shaped God of War Ragnarök: Valhalla](https://sms.playstation.com/stories/how-kratos-past-shaped-god-of-war-ragnarok-valhalla/media/1100) and [Shaping Valhalla's Narrative](https://sms.playstation.com/stories/shaping-valhallas-narrative-with-kratos-past/media/1120). Developers directly contrast Helios and Mimir on Kratos's belt as guilt versus loving support, and describe Valhalla's acceptance of multiple perspectives. Preserved logical owners `character_sources/Black_Mimir_Narration_Source.txt` and `research/GOW_Baldur_Kratos_Fidelity_20261018.md` remain full fidelity routes where not superseded.

E32 `No Horizon` DELIVERED_PROVISIONAL not accepted; next E33. WORK advances zero story.

Dana and Chris should genuinely play Red Dead Redemption 2 together **more often**. This is a recurring changing friendship, not one line about games, a mandatory episode-opening, or a side quest. Dana's high-honor preference, practical protectiveness, craft judgment, teasing and willingness to instigate contrast with Chris's chaotic obsession with looting nearly every body, wandering off the mission, poker, horse antics, greet–greet–antagonize, lack of concern for the honor bar, fierce save-file protection and unexpectedly sincere attachment to the Van der Linde characters. **LATEST DIRECT AUTHOR LOCK: Dana LIKES HIGH-HONOR RDR2 PLAYTHROUGHS. Chris DOES NOT GIVE A SHIT ABOUT HONOR.** This is a current preferred playstyle, not an assertion of specific completed game missions or save state. Dana may break from his preferred style in an earned moment, but the contrast must be a recognizable recurring source of banter and meaningful decisions. He might protect an NPC, notice a badly built bridge or horse behavior, deliberately provoke Chris, or embrace chaos himself depending on his own judgment. Their competition should generate decisions, jokes, irritation, affection and genuine learned respect, not permanent morality stereotyping.

Only the original **BELLY interior** RDR2 setup exists. Dana's return from currently unresolved E32 position and any voluntary entrance require real geography and Billy-controlled bodily access. Dana is 8 feet 10 inches and approximately 840 lb in base; furniture, doorway, floor, game controller and sight lines must support actual physical use, without resizing him or materializing a second console. Chris can play only when full-sized in BELLY; he cannot control the internal game while physically exterior. If Billy is exterior, Chris can be inside and Dana may be a lawful, voluntary guest by an actually supported route. Existing BELLY resident camera, acid, storage and visitor protections all remain.

Establish what the screen actually shows and what Chris/Dana do, rather than inventing a previous shared save, a specific current mission, a pre-existing score competition or an NPC relationship. Charles/Meatwad and eventually Shake/Frylock can be irritating co-residents when actually present, never forced as a reaction chorus.
