# TPP: absurd criminal rivals with the *whole* Season 3 world available
**Work-only character/story opportunity guide, NOT enacted E33 scenes or guaranteed outcomes.** Current direct author: `continuity/author/Deadpool_Primary_Narrator_TPP_Worldwide_Rivalry_After_E32_20261019.json`. Last delivered E32 `No Horizon`. All physical arrivals/contacts require actual locations, information routes, independent choices, time and resources.

## TPP is a rival gang, not a joke cutaway

The existing TPP was formed E12, has rented headquarters/rent pressures, a real E17 tattoo disaster, a conjoined Tracy Morgan and original Brian, self-aggrandizing Dutch, revenge-obsessed Clifford (same adult Jack), and by E32 an actual stolen **Noxian harbor redirected-cargo declaration ledger** obtained during the evacuation scam; one woman got a refund and other ticket liabilities are unresolved. These are tangible things TPP can act on, not an automatic vault of profits or omniscient intelligence. Tracy does NOT consent to a life in TPP and is furious about the Busta misidentification. Claudia? No: use actual **Clifford**. No separate John Marston or actual Vader. 

Zid's betrayal and joining TPP is **author-selected future**, not retroactively completed. He remains Sid-dominant fused Zion/Sid with a desperate need to be recognized and a history of sometimes useful mistakes. When the betrayal is enacted, make its disloyalty costly and psychologically earned, give TPP members independent reactions and let Zid's petty jealousy fuel specific anti-Sins proposals. Zid isn't a genius spy and cannot spontaneously know BELLY layouts, Sins private funds, every single power or a Noxian official's phone number. He can use things he ACTUALLY learned as a Sin and real physical observations—some memories can be wrong.

Five new author-selected TPP members: Cartman as The Coon, Jimmy, Timmy, Dr. Evil, Austin Powers. Their admissions/encounters have to happen on page with physical access; adult hero Austin might refuse specific abuse or leave, Dr. Evil/Dutch/Cartman can fight over command, Jimmy can refuse to be the clown, Timmy can make surprising independent choices. They retain real ages/abilities. No sexual situations with the children. Each is a distinctive person, not a shared gang laugh track.

## Proposed elaborate anti-Sins plots: optional starting points, not scheduled completions

### The Grand Serpent Maritime Safety Monopoly
**Want:** Dutch needs legitimate-looking shipping fees and rent money; Zid wants to make the Sins look like reckless invaders; Cartman wants licensed superhero credit; Dr. Evil wants an enormous ceremonial headquarters.
**Actual seed:** E32 real cargo ledger with redirected shipments plus the actual World Serpent's body and damaged harbor. Requires an actual port/customs contact, guards' knowledge and paperwork, and physical route—not magical new authority.
**Idiot mechanism:** Rename a dockside salvage tent the Official Anti-Snake Maritime Authority; sell fake safe-passage passes, absurdly specific body-part liability waivers and certificates stamped with a giant drawing of the serpent's anus. Brian demands a licensing fee for the logo; Tracy correctly objects to signing as Busta and refuses to take the ticket booth; Dutch insists the fee structure is a visionary freedom movement. Zid proposes positioning signs where the Sins might arrive.
**Collision:** A real Noxian warmason, naval pilot, corrupt official, Bilgewater captain or stranded merchant may refuse, expose, buy or take over the fraudulent service. The serpent is intelligent and may move independently, making the "safe" route dangerous.
**Consequences:** Some fees may be collected, actual victims may demand refunds, Noxians investigate, or a better equipped pirate steals the ledger. Nothing is guaranteed by outline.

### Project Fake Sins Family Reunion
**Want:** Zid seeks the sweet humiliation of making his old friends look gullible. Clifford wants Arthur embarrassed. Brian wants TPP's name on the invitation. Dutch wants a buyer's money and a distracting spectacle.
**Means:** Only after Zid is actually accepted, access to a recently established **public** Sin reputation detail and an actually reachable venue, not an invented gang base or secret BELLY map.
**Idiot mechanism:** A giant counterfeit banquet/family reunion, paid actors claiming to be gang associates, forged “loyalty certificates,” a criminal auction and a statue of Kaze sculpted from cheap confiscated candle wax. Zid insists the roast is surgical psychological warfare, yet cannot explain who is supposed to attend or why. Tracy refuses to be the musical headliner. Dr. Evil wants to charge admission to the execution that hasn't happened. Jimmy may be the only person checking the logistics.
**Collision:** Real witnesses or any available Sins may ignore it, exploit the idiots' stage, expose counterfeits or turn the attempted humiliation against rivals. Other crossover visitors can pursue the fake auction for their OWN goals, not because the list demands a cameo.
**Cost:** Fake tickets, costume labor, rented space, ruined reputations, real insult debts. Hilarious scheme may even win money before collapsing.

### Operation Grandfather's Revenge: the Walking Clifford Launch
**Want:** Clifford wants to walk again and make the Sins afraid; Dutch wants the dramatic comeback of a mythic gunslinger; Dr. Evil wants a revenue stream from villainy; Zid wants to help for approval.
**Means:** Author-approved future shoddy Vader-like surgery must actually be staged, tools/doctor/repair parts/physically credible mobility mechanisms established, and the patient revealed as Clifford, not Anakin. No free spinal regrowth.
**Idiot mechanism:** Market Clifford's reconstruction as "Darth Clifford: The Return of the Refund"; steal a projected silhouette, dramatic breathing machine and cheap black metal mask. Tracy shouts that nobody hired him to play Busta at the launch. Brian declares himself executive Sith producer. The Coon claims the theatrical vigilante rights.
**Collision:** Arthur and Chris remember Clifford's actual history, though not future scenes. A Noxian surgeon, Starkiller, Maul, Austin Powers or real war veterans might react independently if genuinely present. The fake costume cannot grant actual Star Wars powers. A credible first use of his new legs can be genuinely dangerous even if the helmet makes a fart noise.
**Cost:** Recovery, pain, funding, power supply, and resentment; no automatic victory or forced rematch.

### The World's Worst Counter-Bounty
**Want:** Zid wants revenge/recognition, Brian wants corporate structure, Dutch needs payment, Dr. Evil enjoys elaborate formal threats. Cartman wants an embossed bounty on people who insulted him.
**Means:** Real witnessed crimes, legitimate/illegitimate buyers who actually reach TPP, and knowledge particular targets are alive in reachable areas.
**Idiot mechanism:** Sell contradictory overlapping bounties on Sins' masks, knees, furniture, liquor bottles and hypothetical “classified hair samples”; hire mercenaries through a complicated fake reverse auction whose contract language forces the winning bidder to pay TPP instead. Dr. Evil explains the auction in a forty-slide presentation; Tracy walks out as far as their shared body allows, taking Brian with him.
**Collision:** Samira may reject an insulting contract; Deadpool is narrator-only from E33 and **cannot physically accept or mock** a TPP offer; another genuinely reachable contractor must respond independently; Darius can detain fraudsters; Bilgewater pirates may decide TPP is easier to rob than the Sins. Buyers keep agency and valid combat strengths.
**Cost:** Reputation, money, real retaliation and possible permanent injuries. An elaborate plan does not auto-nerf Sins or surrender allies.

## World intersection lanes
- **Noxus locals**: Swain could investigate actual shipping/soldier reports; Darius could prioritize troop logistics; Draven might seek a spectacle; Katarina/Talon require real contracts and access; LeBlanc/Vladimir/Elise have private goals; Samira may pursue better paying work or reject TPP. Each knows only reports received.
- **Port/sea**: Bilgewater pirates, Buhru, serpent hunters, Nami/other aquatic people when accessible, Pyke and Nautilus under their exact source restrictions. Vessel, depth and coast matter.
- **Zaun/Piltover**: Heisenberg's enterprise and independents may compete for stolen cargo only via an actual trade/transport path.
- **Crossover cast**: surviving Magneto, Thor, Kratos, Baldur, Tai Lung, Riddick, Maul/Shang Tsung/Starkiller, selected future Woody/Buzz, penguins, Johnny Bravo, Shake/Frylock, Courage and Daffy may meet TPP if self-driven paths intersect. Deadpool has NO physical actor appearances from E33, and his narration is not an in-universe witness service.
- **Darkin and regions**: hosts/objects and danger restrictions govern; do not make Darkin instant TPP bidders or men magically present. Female native roster remains independent, and Kaze romance OFF remains active; no compatibility matchmaking quotas.
- **Sins**: Chris/Billy share one exterior body, Dana/Mana loyal, Arthur full toolkit, Daniel/Kaze agency and real earnings; Zid's betrayal is his own. Plans should trigger a *real* victim response; don't shrink Sins' established kit to make clown criminals competitive.

## High energy without boring repetition
A crazy plan should escalate through **three different person's bad decisions**, expose at least one genuinely competent counter-move, and end on a change (payment, object custody, injury, audience reputation, lost trust) where appropriate. No fixed three-beat quota: the rule is causal richness. Tracy's furious refusal can derail profitable plans; Brian can know one obscure technical thing; Jimmy can be right; Dutch can deliver a persuasive speech; children can leave or object; Zid can be laughably stupid and *still* hurt the Sins. Don't turn him into an unstoppable genius or force TPP to lose every encounter. Preserve actual physical and money continuity.

Source/canon truth above is current S7NS author intent and delivered E32 facts; proposal sections are **pure prospective original planning**.

# S7NS Season 3: Noxus and crossover character decision gates

**Scope:** Source-based editorial application inside the existing `continuity/SEASON3_ACTIVE_OUTLINE.json`. Not a new canon authority, episode, fight schedule, enacted scene or guaranteed outcome. Main outline and current delivered checkpoint govern; full individual owners must be read before consequential deployment.

**Current boundary:** E32 `No Horizon` delivered provisional, not author-accepted. E33 next and not yet played. Dana and Mana restored as loyal Sins, location not yet evidenced. Zid betrayal/TPP entry author-selected but not enacted. Living characters can meet one another or the Sins again after actual access. Events written as “could” are not offscreen history.

## Noxian decisions, not stage entrances

**Swain.** Wants a capable durable empire, truthful strategic intelligence and advantages over hidden internal enemies. Courtly questions can conceal ruthless calculations, and his demon-sourced reports are incomplete. A credible marine/port report can make him compare testimony and choose a bounded response. An incorrect decision may genuinely cost him. He doesn't know every crossover mind or watch all ravens. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Swain_Character_Source.txt`.

**Darius.** Values legion discipline, loyalty, imperial cohesion and his living brother Draven. He can respect capability and still reject unpredictable recruits. A disrupted supply route might make him choose troops, evacuation, patrol discipline or refusal rather than an arbitrary duel. Do not impose grief for the erased old-branch Draven death. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Darius_Character_Source.txt`.

**Draven.** Craves the public spectacle that sustains his standing, but remains technically lethal. A prestigious outsider could be publicity, a rival, or an unwelcome diversion. Audience and venue must actually be present; he doesn't fight every import on schedule. Darius relationship remains active. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Draven_Character_Source.txt`.

**LeBlanc.** Works to preserve the Black Rose's centuries of leverage, against Swain and other genuine threats. Her patience, layered disguises and interrogation can fail; secret information needs a located carrier and a real source. She does not orchestrate all rival operations or control all outsiders. Owner: `character_sources/season3/LeBlanc_Character_Source.txt`.

**Vladimir.** Wants age-resistant influence, private predation and personally meaningful memory, not just a fight. Courteous concern can be exploitative; his old alliance with LeBlanc isn't perfect identity of interests. Foreign Darkin/alien biology isn't automatically his to manipulate. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Vladimir_Character_Source.txt`.

**Elise.** Wants to maintain status and youth through intimate manipulations and predation, which can break when a victim learns enough. Her E25 Kaze history persists without granting her gang membership or compulsory return. A faction contact needs real elite access. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Elise_Character_Source.txt`.

**Katarina.** Duty, family conflict, competitive pride and chosen desire can coexist. She left E31 with evidence of the governor plot; E32 confirmed a mutual kiss with Kaze. The warning has not magically arrived at its destination. A kiss neither terminates her mission nor guarantees sex, allegiance or further knowledge. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Katarina_Character_Source.txt`.

**Talon.** Practical assassin whose source-era allegiance and Du Couteau tensions require precision. He might obey, conceal, betray or decline an actual assignment; do not make him Swain's universal scout or Katarina's silent satellite. His concealment doesn't erase all scent/sound. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Talon_Character_Source.txt`.

**Riven.** Remorse, competence, protective love and loyalty conflict after the Ionian war. She can protect, flee or defy a real obligation without becoming a helpless captive or automatically teleporting into a Noxian arena. Establish whether she currently possesses the broken runic sword. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Riven_Character_Source.txt`.

**Samira.** Her chosen thrills and independence are stronger than abstract imperial patriotism. She left south after E29 with restored specialized gear and a still-braced wrist; current repair/arrival are unknown. She is an existing Kaze partner, not a Sin or a default travel companion; her Arthur grievance survives. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Samira_Character_Source.txt`.

**Briar.** An adult Black Rose-created hemomantic person whose hunger for connection and control of her frenzy make freedom paramount. She departed north in E28 and is currently author-held offstage; previous Kaze intimacy persists. A list of possible meetings cannot override this hold. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Briar_Character_Source.txt`.

**Sion.** An abused reanimated Noxian war weapon with fragments of older self and unstable lucidity; he endangers allies as well as enemies. A commander must decide whether a credible military need warrants risking his release. He is not a contract soldier patiently awaiting a scheduled Sin duel. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Sion_Character_Source.txt`.

## Displaced and independent crossover people

**Thor.** Wants family/home and control over when violence defines him. E32 he chose not to throw Mulenear while watching Yormungander and turned inland; his next route-home action needs a credible lead. No obligatory second Thor-serpent fight. Owner: `character_sources/Thor_Character_Source.txt`.

**Jörmungandr (TTS Yormungander).** LATEST DIRECT AUTHOR OVERRIDE: the OLDER, 2018 Lake-of-Nine mature serpent, with genuine prior Mimir/Kratos/Atreus experiences and Thor enmity, is the ONE actual world-encircling serpent in Runeterra. E32 witnessed harbor/Bilgewater body sections and independent choices remain. No second serpent, new import, universal language, omniscient port reports or added weather magic. Current full owner: `character_sources/Jormungandr_Character_Source.txt` and newest author override.

**Heimdall.** DEAD in current branch E15. Source contempt/Odin loyalty survives only as informed historical knowledge and consequences; no new living duel. Owner: `character_sources/Heimdall_GOW_Character_Source.txt`.

**Deadpool.** After his completed physical E19 paid introduction, the latest author removes his physical role entirely from E33 onward. He is ONLY the ordinary first-person main narrator, with film-authentic Wade personality, an optional 'Marvel Jesus' introduction and wild reactive rants. No further physical job, Sins/TPP/Noxian encounter or actor intelligence. Preserve earlier embodied history. Current owner: `canon/presentation/Deadpool_Primary_Narration_After_E32_20261019.md`; direct update `continuity/author/Deadpool_NarratorOnly_Dana_Chris_RDR2_After_E32_20261019.json`.

**Tai Lung.** Alive and injured after E13 defeat, with recognition/mastery wounds rooted in Shifu's rejection. He can adapt or double down on entitlement, but a future challenge needs time to recover and a genuinely reachable reason. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Tai_Lung_Character_Source.txt`.

**Maul.** At the selected Clone Wars S7 instant, domination by Sidious and dread of what comes next can motivate leverage, manipulation and risky recruitment. He must acquire local information before promising it; no imported Shadow Collective army, Rebels experience or forced Starkiller partnership. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Maul_Character_Source.txt`.

**Shang Tsung.** Self-serving bargain-maker pursuing power/knowledge through source-valid expertise. He may offer a small useful service, mislead a buyer or walk away when his actual capability fails. No Well mastery, Titan force, mind control of Darkin by default. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Shang_Tsung_Character_Source.txt`.

**Lord Starkiller.** Reconstructed Sith Stalker driven by fear, enforced obedience and dangerous longing for autonomy. He can reject an apparent new master or bargain poorly; neither Maul nor a Noxian automatically commands him. No hero-ending history, TFU2 clone or all DLC memories. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Lord_Starkiller_Character_Source.txt`.

**Riddick.** Survival, wary pragmatism and chosen attachment after betrayal; no automatic loyalty to powerful Noxians or the Sins. Locally study an actual environment before acting on it. Exact current place/gear remains unverified. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Riddick_Character_Source.txt`.

**Magneto.** Guilt, disappointment and renewed resolve can inform the community he chooses to protect. He independently defended refugees in E30 and can encounter the Sins or a Noxian leader again if routes intersect. He is not their escort or a generic metal-thrower; selected issue interior access remains incomplete. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Magneto_Character_Source.txt`.

**Kratos.** Ragnarok/Valhalla-era restraint, history of violence, and autonomy endure. After E26 he traveled toward quarry hills; an independent next decision needs genuine access and motive, not another duel because he's famous. Research: `research/GOW_Baldur_Kratos_Fidelity_20261018.md`.

**Baldur.** Alive with unbroken numbness curse after E22 encounter/E26 escape report. Obsessive hunger to feel and hostility can drive new movement, but a rematch must be reached and chosen. He is not automatically cured by Kaze or a Noxian sorcerer. Research: `research/GOW_Baldur_Kratos_Fidelity_20261018.md`.

**Dutch van der Linde.** Independent TPP rival with late Chapter 3 persona, Bob Ross grief, need for money/attention/loyalty, persuasive self-deception. E32 his crew stole a harbor declarations ledger while the harbor was in crisis; he needs a real downstream buyer/plan. Clifford, Busta and tiny Brian have separate wants. Rent remains unresolved; no imported Dutch gang. Owner: `character_sources/season3/Van_der_Linde_Dutch_Source.txt`.

## Every choice must pass the same evidence boundary

A person's *motivation* is not a guarantee they win, arrive, ally, betray, seduce, die or fight. First locate them at their source era, keep their kit and own history intact, establish what they know, what they want today and why another person is in the way, then let them choose and live with consequences. Family, debt, fraud, coercion, professional pride, spiritual duty, trauma, desire and boredom should not collapse into the same universal motivation.

**Extended/regional actors:** the individually scoped 112-entry roster and 70-entry women/age/region map are preserved in the project's S7NS Library; this guide is not a replacement for their complete source owners. Especially preserve Kayn/Rhaast contested single-body mechanics, darkin weapon-host gates, dead ancient Darkin and discontinued ballista, independent TPP rivalry and the author-directed loyal return of Dana and Mana. No automatic woman's hookup or regional teleport.

## Current all-roster crossover permission and Clifford parody
Every surviving source-valid crossover may cross paths with other crossovers, Noxus or Runeterra people, TPP and Sins when actual routes/motives permit. A first scene does not retire someone. Deadpool is narrator-only from E33 and NOT physically available. Clifford's future off-brand Spaceballs-like Darth Vader walking surgery can create a genuinely awkward future interaction with Lord Starkiller (who knows Vader) and Maul (pre-Vader-era and NOT automatically familiar with Vader costume). No meeting, recovery or surgical result is delivered merely by this permission.

# S7NS — consolidated QAPX handoff (staged; NOT installed)

**Authority:** Latest direct author decisions in this conversation through the instructions on Mimir/Kratos, Chris's snack stash and Clifford's neck/tattoos. This is a practical owner-and-route reconciliation, not another rewrite or season reset. The 2026-10-17–19 original notes, episodes, Specials, manuscripts, research and historical bundle files remain preserved.

**Branch:** `S3_RESET_CAMPFIRE_CANON_CHRIS_RETOLD_20261017`. **Latest delivered:** S3 E32, *No Horizon*, delivered provisionally, **NOT author-accepted**. The E32 checkpoint's `PREPARED` label is historical; the E32 `DELIVERY_RECONCILIATION.json` confirms delivery. **Next:** E33 NOT written / NOT played. WORK advances no story. Latest independent checkpoint before this work: `continuity/work/RESUME_CHECKPOINT_20261019_POST_E32_CANON_RECONCILIATION.json` (seven post-E32 mechanics corrections). Check for newer actual matching-branch delivery or owner changes before cutover.

## Latest three corrections — new current owners

1. **Mimir & Kratos:** Same formerly narrating Black Mimir is now the **actual reanimated head hanging on post-Valhalla Kratos's hip**. Deadpool alone narrates ordinary S7NS main episodes from E33; Billy retains first-person campfire Specials. Kratos and Mimir must match the post-Valhalla game psychology, trust, disagreements, language and ability/physical-sense limits. Mimir is not a second duplicate or omniscient remote narrator. Kratos was last seen moving toward quarry hills E26; his new travel since is not invented. New owner: `canon/characters/Kratos_Mimir_Post_Valhalla_Companions_20261019.md` and direct override `continuity/author/Mimir_Kratos_Chris_Stash_Clifford_Visual_QAPX_After_E32_20261019.json`. Santa Monica Studio's Valhalla retrospective supports the Mimir-on-belt relationship and Kratos's changed receptive judgment. It does NOT supply new Runeterran travel or events.
2. **Chris's private BELLY food:** Pepsi in plastic **BOTTLES** (not cans), **Reese's peanut butter** (retain author's wording; do not silently assume candy cups) and **Eyeholes cereal**, stock quantity unspecified, accessible in actual domestic hoard, not endless spawned food. Fictional Charles's established Pepsi/peanut-butter thievery persists. Shake/Frylock are FUTURE roommates, not retroactive thieves. Source: `canon/characters/Chris_Food_Stash_20261019.md` plus `character_sources/Chris_Character_Source.txt` and BELLY owner.
3. **Clifford visual:** SAME adult Jack/Clifford Marston, not John. In **every actual on-screen appearance**, physically **bent/crooked neck** from the retained repeated Chris/Arthur cliff shoves/falls, along with rough **incomplete Chris-and-Arthur stick-and-poke tattoos**; also distinct unfinished **TP** letters from delivered E17 gang tattoo. No invented precise tattoo content/neck angle, no automatic cosmetic repair by future fake-Vader walking reconstruction. He uses the actual current wheelchair until surgery is PLAYED. Owner: `character_sources/crossover_extended/Clifford_Marston_Visual_20261019.md` plus prior complete adult Jack owner and research.

## Reconciled decisions from this entire conversation

### Source architecture / playback
- Do not reinstall, reset, duplicate workspace or restart the season. Full original owners through `MANIFEST.yaml` + `routing/MASTER_SOURCE_INDEX.json` + `complete_sources/ACCESS_INDEX.json`; indices route, do not replace full owners. All prior S1–S2, five Specials and S3 E1–32 exact manuscripts remain in place. Latest author instruction wins scoped conflicts. The installed single story procedure is `canon/presentation/Season3_Scene_Decision_Doctrine_20261010.md` (entire manuscript draft, full read, factual/editorial repair, entire final reread, observed passage/source comparisons). TTS 3,750–4,500 episode-prose words at 150 assumed wpm; length earned through eventful scenes, not filler. Delivered ≠ accepted.
- Later restored source owners and GitHub cutover routing supersede old frozen physical module headings; **Project custom instructions and the 28 original physical bundle files were NOT shown replaced/readback-verified by the current work**. Preparing QAPX now must not claim they are installed.

### Cast and full individual personalities
- **The Sins** are separately motivated chosen-family criminals, not a single commander and four joke sidekicks. They take self-chosen robbery/mercenary/salvage/revenge opportunities; participating job-leader's net split 60%, other actual participants 40% when applicable. Gear, earned money and unallocated coin, witness knowledge, injuries, regional retaliation, grief and interpersonal frictions have future consequences. No equal-action, power or joke quota.
- **Kaze** retains 40 behavioral mechanisms, adult established relationship history and his full *current* earned Darkin/CASA/BLACKLIGHT + right EMS/Black Fire + complete Shrine/Warwick/soul kit via Black Marrow. NO Rinnegan, BLACKCURRENT, Saiyan/Majin/Buu, ungranted left EMS/Susanoo, substitute portals or extra weapon copies. Never drinks. Romance **OFF**, although earlier consensual partner relations and author-selected future pregnancies remain historical/prospective, not discovered by actors or automatically new dates. Briar completed northward/offstage departure and her intimacy persists; don't force her back.
- **Dana and Mana** explicitly reinstated LOYAL, **NEVER BETRAYED**. Earlier global removal superseded. Full original owner/male identity/earnings/progression restored; Dana's protector/builder/Brok voice, gamma/Kahuhu and forms; Mana quadrupedal nonverbal blue-nose pit bull and independent partner. # S7NS — DEADPOOL AS PRIMARY NARRATOR: SOURCE-GROUNDED FILM SPEECH FIDELITY + SELECTIVE RICK-STYLE CONTEMPT
Scope: WORK only; source-era film Deadpool after the *Deadpool & Wolverine* 2024 apartment reunion. Latest author explicitly replaces Black Mimir as the ordinary main-episode narrator, retaining Deadpool's authentic voice and adding *select* Rick Sanchez-like contempt, absurd digression and scalpel-sharp social critique. This is a writing profile and scope-bound authoring recommendation, not an audio voice clone or claim an E33 episode exists.

## A. Verified source corpus and method
1. **Deadpool (2016)**: Rhett Reese / Paul Wernick 16 Nov 2015 final-shooting screenplay (readable complete 118-page script HTML); corroborated performed-dialogue transcript. https://overblack.org/script/deadpool-2016 ; https://www.springfieldspringfield.co.uk/movie_script.php?movie=deadpool . Read taxi/driver exchange, freeway hijack/opening first audience aside, tracking vengeance and ammo, Colossus's moral opposition and Vanessa/Francis emotional turning points. The text captures dialogue and staging, *not acoustic pitch or an audition*.
2. **Deadpool 2 (2018)**: publicly transcribed on-screen dialogue. https://www.springfieldspringfield.co.uk/movie_script.php?movie=deadpool-2 . Inspected opening Wolverine self-interested movie-spoof and non-linear "family film" framing, Dopinder's wish to belong, Vanessa's death and grief, Russell/Firefist rescue dilemma, X-Force recruitment bravado and humiliating reversal, final prolonged self-dramatized dying farewell and found-family resolution. Since transcript is not official screenplay, minor wording/attribution may have transcription errors.
3. **Deadpool & Wolverine (2024)**: performed-dialogue transcription, https://www.springfieldspringfield.co.uk/movie_script.php?movie=deadpool-wolverine ; source-owner separately attests prior Disney released screenplay reading with draft/final differences. Analyzed TVA/Paradox self-worth exchange, low-status embarrassment about Avengers, "worst Wolverine" reversal, Nicepool/Dogpool feud, movie-industry winks, appalling Honda Odyssey grievance turning into antagonism and physical clash, Cassandra memory distortion (NOT reliable Vanessa speech), compassion toward friends, Logan's furious attack ending Wade's chatter, two-person sacrifice and closing apartment reunion. Don't import variant equipment/TemPad or Marvel timeline powers.
4. **Creator's view — 2017 Writers Guild East OnWriting**: https://www.wgaeast.org/onwriting/rhett-reese-and-paul-wernick-deadpool/ . Writers directly describe irreverence, self-loathing, pathos, underdog aspect, non-linearity and how emotionally grounded relationships make the genre-rejection work.
5. **Deadpool 2 writers — Motion Picture Association 2018**: https://www.motionpictures.org/2018/05/deadpool-2s-screenwriters-on-living-with-wade-wilsons-voice-in-their-heads/ ; https://www.comingsoon.net/movies/news/827621-exclusive-deadpool-2-writers-talk-cable-and-more . They emphasize uniquely personal stakes, family/heart, faster joke construction without requiring world-saving scale.
6. **Deadpool & Wolverine co-writer Zeb Wells 2024**: https://www.motionpictures.org/2024/08/deadpool-wolverine-co-writer-zeb-wells-on-scripting-marvels-raunchiest-wildest-film-ever/ . Deadpool lines must propel story AND character while being funny; emotional relief permits vulnerable pauses.
7. **Ryan Reynolds and co-writers on fourth-wall rules**: https://www.vanityfair.com/hollywood/story/deadpool-wolverine-ryan-reynolds-hugh-jackman-shawn-levy ; Ryan in Nov 2024 Scriptnotes https://johnaugust.com/2024/scriptnotes-episode-657-deadpool-with-ryan-reynolds-transcript . Reynolds says only Wade's fourth-wall awareness helps other characters remain dramatically true, and emphasizes Logan/Wade reversal when Logan gets the extended offensive tirade and Wade shuts up.
8. **Editing rhythm**: Deadpool 2 writers on cut/alternate gags and emotional center, https://www.esquire.com/entertainment/movies/a20749870/deadpool-2-writers-paul-wernick-rhett-reese-interview/ ; https://www.denofgeek.com/movies/rhett-reese-and-paul-wernick-interview/ . Writer recognition that constant yapping becomes irritating supports deliberate silence rather than word-counted banter.
9. **Rick & Morty creator context**: Dan Harmon discusses Rick's stable cynicism and occasional concealed feeling: https://www.theguardian.com/tv-and-radio/2015/jul/30/ricky-and-morty-creators-were-pulling-apart-the-tropes-of-sci-fi ; structural surrealism grounded in family https://www.wired.com/video/watch/wired-live-community-s-dan-harmon-talks-about-his-adult-swim-show-rick-and-morty ; primary interview https://undertheradarmag.com/interviews/rick_and_mortys_dan_harmon_and_justin_roiland . Rick behavioral traits here are *influence*, not an assertion Wade used Rick's actual dialogue.
10. **Installed S7NS owners read in full from sha-verified bundled bytes**: `character_sources/season3/Deadpool_Character_Source.txt`, `character_sources/Black_Mimir_Narration_Source.txt`, `canon/presentation/PRESENTATION.md`, `canon/presentation/Season3_Scene_Decision_Doctrine_20261010.md`, `topic_sources/Group_Relationships.txt` and `topic_sources/Ensemble_Scene_Application.txt`. The baseline Deadpool owner already contains a **completed narrow E19 narration hijack** and post-reunion psychological pin. This new author decision supersedes its "hijack ONLY" limitation *prospectively*, never rewriting that completed moment.
The research uses scripts/dialogue transcripts + firsthand interviews. It is an extensive **text-based voice and writing-mechanism study**, NOT a claim to have watched every complete film with measured vocal cadence, performed an actor-voice analysis or exhausted Marvel comics.

## B. Film-to-film performed voice: specific mechanics
**2016 — The merc talking past his own hurt.** One dialogic thought starts with true affection or a practical job and is deliberately defaced by a grotesque bodily metaphor. Conversational pivot sequence: intimate reassurance -> improbable comparison -> a listener's naive question -> impossibly disgusting specificity -> immediate reorientation toward the real operation. Taxi chatter and the Colossus contrast make this audible. He often corrects official stories with his own selfish version, misrepresents motive, then accidentally reveals his pain. His image of himself is slippery and vulnerable, not an omnipotent stand-up god. The opening uses fractured chronology and a direct camera address to create a conspiratorial relationship with the audience, not to explain every action in advance.

**2018 — Framing lies and a human wound.** Opens by mocking the superhero/franchise formula and calls himself a family-film hero as carnage follows. The joke pays off as real loyalty to Russell and his makeshift family. Wade uses self-congratulatory heroic rhetoric, personalizing every moment, but his protection of a vulnerable kid becomes serious. His socially inappropriate observations are fast, startling and frequently both cruel and affectionate. His prolonged "dying" has childish audience hunger and real attachment in the same breath. His bad decisions cause harm and should remain consequential; regenerative ability isn't a comedy stop-clock for others. Family is a deep motive, not generic "never cares."

**2024 — Post-reunion source snapshot.** His self-worth is bruised by rejected heroism and estrangement; loneliness/mattering energizes showmanship. He can be confrontational over an absurdly specific piece of junk (the Honda Odyssey), treating it like a civilization-ending injustice, and he can stop mid-joke when someone he cares about might die. Wolverine's blistering minivan speech is the scene's emotional high point *because Wade actually goes silent*. He is simultaneously mean, performative, crass, attached, reactive and insecure. His fandom/meta comments are sharp but subject to a fourth-wall convention; they do not grant all timeline knowledge or the secrets of a Runeterran he hasn't met.
Selected S7NS pin remains **after 2024 apartment reunion**, not opening depressed salesman Wade. The E19 paid Noxian connection remains delivered but no repeated "first arrival."

## C. Speech engine to implement in S7NS narration
- **Audience complicity**: speaks to the listener as confidant/co-conspirator, teases their expectation and acknowledges things the narrative itself just demonstrated. Avoid direct self-report of confidential author prompts, tools, hidden manuscript or future canon.
- **Fast pivots with recoverable thread**: offer a concrete observation, escalate to utterly disproportionate vulgar comparison, undercut himself, return cleanly to whose action is changing the stakes. Don't wander so far that a TTS listener forgets who is where.
- **Inverted professionalism**: an absurdly polite announcement describes grotesque crime; a military funeral becomes an apology about how bad the seating is, then the grief is honored. The third beat changes the meaning of the first.
- **Inventive filth**: profanity is uncapped and flexible—crude anatomy, aggressively specific biological impossibilities, petty sexual innuendo among adults, bodily disgust, celebrity misrecognition, mocking bureaucracy, genre roast, death gallows humor. Don't paste the same set of "fuck, asshole, bitch" across every character.
- **Self-interruption and self-own**: fake a grand claim, get caught contradicting himself, acknowledge it without losing his place; relish being caught. A listener, physical incident or character may blow apart his intended punchline.
- **Meta calibration**: comic panels, soundtrack, casting, sequel/review/budget jokes permitted as *Deadpool's address to the audience*. Other characters need not recognize media reference and gain no meta awareness by osmosis. Keep jokes intelligible without fandom prerequisite.
- **Interpersonal edge**: insulting detail comes from the speaker's unique vanity, failure or terrible choice. Deadpool's narrator should hate Dutch's false inspiration, Brian's coercion, Zid's embarrassing incompetence and Tracy's captivity in different voices, not sneer generically at everyone.
- **Unpredictable relief**: trauma or cruelty can bring rare unvarnished sentences. The vulnerable silence that follows matters; no automatic quip at the climax. Wade can misjudge an enemy, be ashamed of a joke and revise his account.
- **Film specificity**: year-aware references to superheroes, action-film clichés, pop culture, comic books and Ryan's own franchise jokes are within the extradiegetic narrator layer; they do NOT retcon same-world awareness into Noxian townspeople.
- **Sound/TTS**: natural conversational contractions, varied sentence lengths, active verbs and coherent people/where/when orientation. Never reproduce Reynolds' recorded voice or require playback of a nonexistent audio performance. No 100-line rapid-fire dialogue ticker; clean paragraphs and timed interruptions.

## D. What to borrow from Rick Sanchez and what NOT to copy
**Borrow:** contempt for idiotic process, precise intellectual takedown, obscene cosmic perspective, pathological impatience, deranged analogy, furious riff that reveals a hidden operational flaw, flat dismissal of ceremonial authority and the ability to turn existential scale into a fight over something petty.
**Do NOT turn Wade into Rick:** No habitual drunken burps/stammers, grandfather/mentor posture, Rick's godlike gadget authority, self-satisfied certainty about all science or world rules, permanent emotional disconnection, habitual direct abuse of a "Morty" stand-in, Rick-specific catchphrases or spoken impersonation. Wade is a self-mocking mercenary who desperately wants chosen family. Rick's bitter brain is a **spice**. Rick himself remains a separate independent crossover actor and a nonmember of Sins unless author separately selects.
**Recommendation:** one recognizable Deadpool narrator with optional Rick-sharpened attack vectors, not two alternating narrator voices and not a featureless 50/50 hybrid.

## E. Exact narrator-role cutover and knowledge limits
**Authority:** The newest user selection makes **Wade Wilson / Deadpool the SOLE ORDINARY narrator** for future main S7NS episodes, beginning next authorized E33. It is a new narrator-role convention that supersedes the older "Black Mimir alone; Wade one brief hijack" limitation. Do not replay the completed hijack or invent another contest where Wade steals it. Earlier delivered E1–32 remain as authored. Black Mimir remains a real canon entity with his full previous knowledge and personality; NOT retroactively killed, removed, transferred into Wade, trapped in a body, forced to narrate or re-voiced as him. Historical Mimir performance remains in those episodes.
**Billy Specials exception:** the separately established first-person Billy campfire/Special self-tellings remain his own until a specific later instruction; ordinary main-episode story narration is Deadpool's.
**Actor versus narrative access:** Wade the physically embodied crossover participant is not magically co-located with every scene or given news, exact body positions, secret BELLY floorplan, later plot turns or people’s internal monologue. The independent Noxian Deadpool can act or reencounter Sins/TPP based on actual choices/travel/knowledge. The narrator convention lets Wade *tell already enacted scenes to the listener*, making bounded fourth-wall jokes and acknowledging their shape, even across intercut strands. This is representational narration, **not a new physical omniscience/camera/telepathy/foresight system**. Only information actually shown in source-true scenes or legitimately obtained is reported as fact; unreliable jokes cannot amend events.
**Tension and reveals:** can be a playful *unreliable interpreter*, not a factual liar about a completed lethal blow/actor knowledge. May withhold an identity fairly for an audience reveal (e.g., future Clifford surgical fakeout). No clairvoyant prophecy, invisible stagehands, plot armor or cheat knowledge.
**Earned 140 techniques:** The existing `canon/presentation/PRESENTATION.md` has 140 author-approved optional techniques, not an invocation quota. Transfer their optional use to Wade's voice; prose approach (nonlinear reveal, focalizer misread, status/rule mismatch, dramatic irony, comic subversion, viewpoint bridge, emotional pause, callbacks, reader misdirection with fair sight/sound) must be genuinely shown through character consequences. Retain sole `canon/presentation/Season3_Scene_Decision_Doctrine_20261010.md` drafting/review method and 3,750–4,500-word/150wpm TTS target, no comedy quota.
**Boundary:** Main story remains source-grounded rather than a self-aware Writer's Room. Deadpool can make a rotten joke about a continuity coincidence; he cannot declare a future pregnancy discovered or use author control to win. Even if he speaks about "the audience" to himself, Noxian characters don't automatically have an audience to address.

## F. Scene-engine blueprint and original (not film) writing calibration
**Narration approach:** 
1. Establish place, actual event and physical stakes clearly.
2. Acknowledge the hideous/ironic fact with a very specific personal vulgar comparison, perhaps an annoying tangent.
3. Compare a participant's claim to observable reality; mock the self-serving contradiction with Rick-sharp surgical focus.
4. Let action or another character overturn Wade's expected joke. Avoid interrupting the significant beating, sacrifice or genuine grief.
5. Return to real consequences, grudges, resources and changed relationships in one clean turn.

**Original demonstration only, not PLAY or script canon**: 
"TPP stole a harbor ledger. Wonderful. Three adults, one hostage by surgical malpractice, a kid who thinks he's Batman with a raccoon budget, and an unpaid landlord walk into maritime commerce. That's not a gang; it's a sexually frustrated committee with a felony punch card. Anyway, the ledger mattered. Which is more than I can say for Dutch's last five fucking speeches."
Adapt when characters have *actually* arrived; the invented example's all-member scene is not currently delivered.

**Film voice test before release:** ask whether a sentence is recognizably Wade rather than generic swearing or Rick's prose; ensure a real emotional want, reference or failure motivates every extended rant; full plot geography stays readable; jokes don't bestow actor knowledge; at least one meaningful serious stretch is allowed unspoiled. Verify with actual scene/source passage comparison and entire reviewed final text.

# S7NS roster fidelity: new TPP and WTF cast (research + proposed application)

**Authority:** `continuity/author/TPP_Tracy_Rage_New_Recruits_WTF_Cast_20261019.json`. S3, after E32 delivered provisional. Research does not enact entrances, powers, physical transfers, surgeries or membership votes.

## Evidence and boundaries
Official South Park pages identify The Coon as 10-year-old Cartman, Jimmy Valmer and Timmy Burch as separate schoolchildren, and Timmy's relatively limited spoken vocabulary (https://southpark.cc.com/w/index.php/Coon ; https://southpark.cc.com/w/index.php?title=Timmy_Burch ; https://southpark.cc.com/w/index.php/Crippled_Summer ). Coon has gadget/urban-superhero parody roles rather than automatically legitimate superpowers. The original franchise’s adult-targeted jokes **do not make the children adults**. Jimmy's speech disability affects cadence but is not his only trait, and Timmy has full intent and selfhood without fluent speech.

The parody mechanics of Dr. Evil and Austin Powers are grounded in Mike Myers interviews/profile: https://time.com/archive/6735699/cinema-austins-power/ ; https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nD-51aLT030 . No full-movie dialogue transcript or measured voice was listened to. Keep their precise sibling-rival history only if source era is chosen in the character's on-page debut.

Pixar official Toy Story bios for Woody/Buzz and their genuine loyalty/competitive history: https://www.pixar.com/toy-story ; https://www.pixar.com/toy-story-3 . Both are animated-size TOYS with pullstring/mechanical limits, not automatically full human bodies or actual interstellar weapons; the Toy Story 1995 version gives Buzz initial delusion of literal rangerhood, later versions show his matured understanding. Choose a supported snapshot in actual debut.

DreamWorks/Universal official `Penguins of Madagascar` (2014): https://prod.dreamworks.com/movies/penguins-of-madagascar ; https://www.universalpicturesathome.com/movies/penguins-of-madagascar . Four distinct penguins, not one collective personality. Adult Swim official ATHF episodes show Master Shake's opportunistic ego, scams, jealousy and cruelty: https://www.adultswim.com/videos/aqua-teen-hunger-force . Courage, Johnny Bravo and Daffy: classic television personalities recognized from series; detailed episode transcripts/creator performance evidence **not fully audited**, and the character applications below are interpretations requiring original owner verification before consequential scenes. No new superpowered feats authorized.

## TPP NEW — Each member's mind, voice and personal leverage

**Eric Cartman — THE COON (child, age ~10).** Status hunger, entitlement, vindictive strategic cruelty, fake moral superiority and unbearable need for credit. He weaponizes invented justice language to launder obvious self-interest; switches from heroic speech to vicious petty grievance when challenged. TPP opportunity: corrupt "safety" branding, fake protection heroics, merchandise, intimidation by proxy; hates sharing billing with Dr. Evil/Dutch. He can deceive, betray and overreach, but does not possess authentic universe-level superpowers just by wearing the costume. Child status demands nonsexual presentation; no adult intimate plot.

**Jimmy Valmer (child).** Eager stand-up entertainer, socially perceptive, ambitious to earn a laugh rather than merely become a joke. His stutter is not a punchline to be reproduced on every word. Timing can include preparation, a stuck syllable and a proper joke payoff; independent good judgment and anger matter. TPP role: stage announcer, public pitch, observational counterweight and someone capable of detecting that a grand scheme is ridiculous. He is not Cartman's unquestioning minion. Respect his crutch/movement limits.

**Timmy Burch (child).** Energetic self-directed boy with wheelchair and limited expressive speech. Vocal vocabulary includes name words and occasional phrases; meaningful communication also takes place via pointing, expression, movement and interaction. Not intellectually empty, not a prop to be rescued, not a supernatural oracle. TPP role: wheel-based mobility ingenuity, independent refusal, disruptive timing, startlingly practical intervention. Special Iron Maiden/Professor Timothy traits and any modified chair depend on selected source version, not automatic maximum game + show power combination. Nonsexual presentation.

**Dr. Evil (adult).** Self-important arch-villain, comic obsession with huge-scale ransom spectacle, maladjusted family intimacy, paternal insecurities and office hierarchy; fond of elaborate but nonsensical institutions. TPP role: pompous rival planner/fake multinational executive. Clash: Dutch sells emotionally moving lies, Dr. Evil demands organizational titles, Cartman refuses any higher-ranked villain, Brian thinks he is the management consultant. Can accidentally make a viable plan, but no automatically imported giant missile base, death ray, space station, Mini-Me, army or money.

**Austin Powers (adult).** Optimistic, flamboyant, sexually confident swinging-era spy with genuine hero instincts, absurd costumes and groovy verbal flourish. Repetition of suggestive quips is not a whole personality. Role: completely mismatched spy/charismatic public face who may join in pursuit of a supposed anti-evil mission, misled about what TPP is; he may leave or actively oppose criminality when he learns it. He is NOT a gleeful evil gangster simply because joining was author-selected. Interpersonal friction with brother/enemy Dr. Evil requires an anchored movie continuity snapshot. Sexual jokes only with adults.

**Tracy Morgan / Brusta Thymes (adult, already physically present).** Actual man **is Tracy Morgan** and his full body is conjoined to fictional Brian's miniature adult torso. Being constantly called Busta Rhymes or the latest variant "Buster Ryans" makes him **genuinely livid, foul-mouthed and increasingly resentful**. Nobody gets an easy happy-go-lucky Morgan impersonation. Brian's claims of managerial ownership and physically inseparable proximity are constant indignities; Dutch and Clifford may aggravate him while failing to notice. He wants recognition as himself and autonomy, actively refuses/undermines plans that exploit him. His goal to get physically free is not an already-performed operation, and cannot be granted by offscreen wish magic. Supplement: `research_sources/Tracy_Morgan_Speech_Fidelity_After_E32_20261018.md`. Full original script history still calls him Busta due to the now-established mistaken identity.

**Brian (fictional adult human, not Family Guy's dog).** Tiny conjoined independent mind with Dr.-Evil-like pomp, entitlement, musical status anxiety and delusional romantic assumptions; genuine vulnerability doesn't excuse treating Tracy as owned. Brian may benefit from genuine cooperation but often worsens their shared position. Dr. Evil's arrival should force a personal status crisis, not an automatic mentorship/friendship.

**Dutch (late Chapter 3 RDR2)** continues grandiosity, pride, self-deception and actual Bob grief. TPP's stolen E32 cargo ledger, rented HQ and unresolved rent remain constraints. He can try to sell an inspiring future but may lose control of very different personalities. **Clifford** remains adult 1914 Jack Marston, resentful and wheelchair-using at E32; off-brand Vader-like walking surgery is prospective, not an already-done power-up. **Zid** betrayal to TPP remains future requiring genuine access.

## NEW S3 CROSSOVER CAST — Body, tension, speech and plausible *own* agenda

**Woody (Toy Story toy sheriff).** Loyal organizer and protector; anxiety about being replaced can make him controlling. Dry western earnestness, animated toy movement and pullstring. He would try to keep Buzz and any other toys safe, not instantly steal gold or accept outlaw leadership. Plausible natural path: merchandise transport or toy collector physically moves him into a port or carnival; he resists someone treating him as property. He is a sentient TOY, not Arthur Morgan sized.

**Buzz Lightyear (Toy Story action figure).** Chivalric, mission-oriented, confident, sometimes gullible and then capable of humility; pop-out wings and tiny figure laser are toy features, not automatically functional starship-scale blasters. Woody partnership/competition is central. A soldier interprets him as an advanced tiny scout; Buzz treats an absurd contract like an interstellar rescue mission. Preserve genre and physical scale, choose before/after discovery snapshot.

**Courage the Cowardly Dog.** Frightened, expressive, vocally stressed but profoundly loyal to Muriel and protective against the unbelievable. Domestic horror becomes funny when Courage alone understands the danger and adults dismiss him. Entry best follows protecting a true loved one or escaping a threat, not willingly becoming gang muscle. Retain cartoonish visual warning behaviors without arbitrary omniscience, teleportation or using him as a sound effect.

**Johnny Bravo.** Vain bodybuilder, upbeat self-worship and perpetually unsuccessful attempts at charming strangers, yet capable of vulnerability or surprising decency. A Noxian social setting gives him real social stakes; he misreads danger and offers absurdly overconfident introductions. Adult flirting only, no automatic success or consent. He is not a genuinely predatory sexual aggressor just because he's stupid and horny.

**Master Shake.** Self-serving, obnoxious, domineering, scheming and routinely incompetent; he treats other people's lives as disposable when convenient but occasionally reveals real neediness. Official ATHF episode summaries ground con artistry and jealousy. Might pursue an independent scam that collides with Dutch, not become Meatwad's replica or be pulled into BELLY by fiat. ATHF abilities/physical limits require his own source snapshot.

**Daffy Duck.** Dramatic self-image, incandescent jealousy, opportunism and desire for top billing, especially around Bugs. He can take a con much too seriously, switch sides for praise and then suffer slapstick consequences. Choose classic Looney Tunes continuity/visual logic; don't automatically grant infinite Toon Force that overrides S7NS danger rules or resurrects all events. Improvised rants work when they reveal panic/vanity, not just noise.

**Penguins of Madagascar (four autonomous actors):**
- **Skipper**: confident tactical commander, suspicious of outsiders, aggressively mission-oriented, surprisingly loyal. Can mistake harbor smuggling for enemy espionage, but may investigate and refuse collateral harm.
- **Kowalski**: analytical, invents devices/theories, often reaches brittle conclusions; considers data a security measure. Science requires materials and time.
- **Rico**: demolition/arsenal specialist with nonstandard vocalizations and stored tools; no infinite object creation; bizarre physical gag can have a specific payoff.
- **Private**: gentle, conscientious, often dismissed as inexperienced, but can make crucial moral and practical decisions.
They operate as independent four-person team rather than generic talking birds, and need actual transport into Runeterra.

## E33 onward placement and precedence

All five added TPP actors **author-selected to JOIN TPP** but only admission through actual scenes; age, mobility, allegiance and adult-only content restrictions intact. One or more can arrive at rival HQ through a supported transport narrative, but no sudden simultaneity nor guaranteed villainous compliance. New crossover characters are **selected for roster participation**, not required first appearance E33, fixed duel or gang membership. Their natural intersections: harbor disrupted by serpent, corrupt merchant cargo, traveling carnival, children's toys in salvage, traveling performers, refugee ships, genuine spy threats. None is confirmed to have been in the E32 harbor already.

Before an episode: retrieve current full owners and exact selected source-era versions; show why person is there, what they want, what they believe, who knows it, what resource they can use, what they refuse and what changes by their choice. No roster quota; not every character needs a scene. All listed personalities are research-backed at varying depth; **a complete original scene-by-scene voice-fidelity review has not been done for every new character**. Preserve Mimir's 140 optional storytelling techniques, nuanced Sins speech, no Kaze romance and living-world causality.

## Source index
South Park official: https://southpark.cc.com/w/index.php/Coon ; https://southpark.cc.com/w/index.php?title=Timmy_Burch ; https://southpark.cc.com/w/index.php/Crippled_Summer
Austin Powers creator/profile: https://time.com/archive/6735699/cinema-austins-power/

# S7NS: catastrophic absurdism, actual transcript observations, and BELLY roommates
WORK only, after delivered E32. No new scene or in-world object already fabricated.

## The user's original S3 Chris-duel sequence
Original logical character/scene owners in uploaded `08_CROSSOVER_EXTENDED(4).md`, `13_SEASON3_CURRENT.md` and `05_CHRIS_BILLY.md` confirm: Micah Bell and actual Bob Ross consciousness in one body, with Bob's devastating exact last words; the apparent Colm O'Driscoll revealed only after fatal blow as disguised fictional Stephen Hawking, whose black-hole threat became diarrhea and introduced one conjoined Brusta/Brian body; Charles the Fish transforming into fictional Charles Manson, later a BELLY household nuisance/warden. The full exact early delivered manuscript was not located during this audit. These are **source-backed event mechanisms**, not new replays or proof of transcript-level accuracy.

## Comic classification and mechanisms
- **Transgressive surrealism / grotesque absurdism**: an astrophysical horror premise yields a scatological anticlimax.
- **Collision-comedy and improbable casting**: men from irreconcilable social/genre backgrounds share one body, raising sustained interpersonal stakes.
- **Anti-comedy / hard tonal reversal**: a heartfelt death, then utter grotesque nonsense, with neither tone needing to announce itself.
- **Deadpan causal impossibility**: Charles Fish -> fictional Charles Manson -> resident/warden. The punchline keeps a job and a physical consequence.
- **Escalating absurdist continuity**: the absurd change persists in housing, grudges, property, relationships, and later plots.
- **Setup-payoff betrayal**: viewers think the scene is entering one genre, then physical, truthful staging reveals an entirely different event.
- **Violent specificity and character-driven disgust**: severe profanity and bizarre insults fit character motivations, but a profanity list without decisions doesn't make the scene funny.

## Rick and Morty script close reading
Sources:
- Pilot transcript: https://rickandmorty.fandom.com/wiki/Pilot_%28Rick_and_Morty%29/Transcript (fan transcription, not official transcript)
- Meeseeks and Destroy transcript: https://rickandmorty.fandom.com/wiki/Meeseeks_and_Destroy/Transcript (fan transcription, not official)
- Rick Potion No. 9 transcript: https://rickandmorty.fandom.com/wiki/Rick_Potion_No._9/Transcript (fan transcription, not official)

The pilot opens with a grotesquely irresponsible emotional coercion amid a wildly futuristic proposition, establishing stakes before plot rules; dialog interruptions let a rational character reveal irrationality. In Meeseeks and Destroy, the simple golf request is impossible to complete for an unwilling Jerry, and the special creatures' inability to exit until success generates a social hierarchy, bureaucratic dispute and physical siege. A petty competence anxiety becomes apocalyptic to beings for whom time is suffering. In Rick Potion No. 9, romantically motivated science causes compound mutation and global damage. Crude comic images emerge from causal misjudgment and specific character weaknesses, not just a string of arbitrary swear words.
Do **not** replicate scripts line for line.

## Trover Saves the Universe research limits and actual sources
A reliable full standalone *Trover* transcript was NOT obtained. Do not claim complete transcript-level research. Used firsthand developer interviews about dialogue development and publicly available examples:
- https://www.gamedeveloper.com/design/making-the-bizarre-hilarious-world-of-i-trover-saves-the-universe-i-
- https://www.unrealengine.com/developer-interviews/trover-saves-the-universe-uses-vr-to-create-one-of-the-funniest-games-in-the-galaxy?lang=de
- https://www.shacknews.com/article/107728/trover-saves-the-universe-interview-justin-roiland-talks-improvisational-design
- https://blog.playstation.com/2019/10/07/trover-saves-the-universe-gets-free-important-cosmic-jobs-dlc-tomorrow/

Trover's structure starts from broad encounter beats, then riff improvisation, branching/interruptible dialogue and editing: developer interview reports roughly 40% of recorded dialogue discarded. NPCs may suddenly respond to player behavior as independent offended/sad individuals; bizarre physiologies and customs are treated as ordinary. Specific vulgar repetitions can rise to prominence, but the writers' craft is in isolating a comic engine and letting people object, interrupt and generate material consequences, with restraint at the climax. Public promotional writing for the Important Cosmic Jobs DLC illustrates the mundane employment-cost frame blown up to apocalyptic proportions. Research applies to structure, not importing Trover's actual game UI or mechanics as S7NS canon.

## BELLY as Chris's hoarder's cave
**Author-selected prospective**: Master Shake and Frylock are to enter BELLY as unwanted roommates with already active Meatwad and Charles. Shake remains an independent ATHF milkshake, narcissistic/greedy, destructive, easily jealous and prone to scams. Frylock is a technologically competent, science-minded sentient box of fries with independent ethics and social irritation. His lasers/levitation and tech capabilities retain source rules rather than automatically granting mastery of BLACKLIGHT/BELLY. Neither is a prisoner by default, a new Sin, or a substitute for Chris/Billy.
- Source for original ATHF tone: https://www.adultswim.com/videos/aqua-teen-hunger-force
- Actual possessions to support new projects: existing hoarded salvaged equipment, food supplies, partial workbench resources and previously established monitors, plus newly demonstrated discovered items. Everything unusual must be obtained rather than spontaneously invented retroactively.
- Possible projects, NOT executed: Shake declares a manufacturing corporation using a box of broken sensors and Chris's stolen signage; Frylock builds an actual circuit diagnostic station only if components exist; Meatwad creates whimsical machinery of dubious function with recovered scraps; Charles attempts a dubious employment scheme through available monitor access. Each absurd project creates a concrete problem for Chris, Billy or another occupant. Acid, prison barriers, and camera restrictions remain.
- The result should be **roommate sitcom meets criminal-storage body horror**, not a weekly obligatory workshop segment. Allow genuine tenderness, independent success, blame and attachment.

## Narrator candidate comparison
Black Mimir remains CURRENT sole ordinary narrator unless author changes it.
- **Rick Sanchez**: best raw profanity and chaotic personal contempt, but may talk over the cast and can't lawfully know scenes unless granted bounded narrator access. He exists as independent character and is not automatically a Sin.
- **The Stanley Parable narrator**: best for unreliable framing and spiraling meta sarcasm, but long moral narration could undercut source-faithful autonomy and risk production-rule jokes.
- **Master Shake**: maximum chaotic self-interest and crude voice, but now also BELLY prospective resident, and his limited firsthand knowledge prevents neutral cross-world overview. Better occasional in-world POV than primary narrator.
- **Black Mimir with a looser personal register**: best continuity and listener orientation; this is not necessarily a new voice, but allowing first-person wrong guesses, precise profane digression and surprise narrator vulnerability should activate existing 140 optional techniques.
- **Deadpool as occasionally bounded narrative interrupter**: fourth-wall-compatible, but original one-time hijack already delivered and should not become an excuse for omniscient cross-scene knowledge. Any new exception requires explicit new authorization.
No permanent replacement selected.

Quality test: source truth, not just reference names; narrative high/low reversals, comic surprise and body logic must change a real person's next action. Save exact passage comparisons in existing whole-text review, not a generic checklist.

<!-- BEGIN LOGICAL OWNER: research/Comedy_Escalation_Profanity_RickMorty_Trover_20261019.md | ROLE: reference_evidence -->
# S7NS researched comedy and high-voltage adult dialogue (WORK, no PLAY)

## Corpus and evidence
- Dan Harmon on the story circle and surrealism anchored by actual relationships: https://nerdist.com/article/rick-and-morty-story-circle-dan-harmon/ ; https://www.latimes.com/entertainment/tv/showtracker/la-et-st-dan-harmon-justin-roiland-on-bringing-the-absurd-to-adult-swims-rick-and-morty-20140304-story.html . Interviews/reporting, not a watch of every season.
- Squanch Games primary designer Erich Meyr on how loose improvised dialogue was shaped around mapped encounter beats: https://www.gamedeveloper.com/design/making-the-bizarre-hilarious-world-of-i-trover-saves-the-universe-i- ; https://www.shacknews.com/article/107728/trover-saves-the-universe-interview-justin-roiland-talks-improvisational-design . Original dialogue grounded in changing player/NPC reactions.
- Trover design/pacing contrasts: https://www.gamespot.com/reviews/trover-saves-the-universe-review-couch-potatoes/1900-6417159/ and https://www.shacknews.com/article/118321/trover-saves-the-universe-1-year-anniversary-interviews-the-dev-team . Some reviewers found excessive unbroken improv monotonous; preserving jokes requires editing, pauses and consequence.
- Rick and Morty structural contrasts, family-stakes and absurd frame: https://www.wired.com/video/watch/wired-live-community-s-dan-harmon-talks-about-his-adult-swim-show-rick-and-morty ; https://time.com/4863924/rick-morty-season-3-dan-harmon-interview/ . Direct sources/coverage are research, not permission to import canon events.
Research scope: inspected accessible excerpts and primary creator statements, plus critical reviews. NOT exhaustive episode-by-episode dialogue transcription or measured timing.

## Comic architecture: broader, wilder, unfiltered
1. **A rotten sensible objective inside impossible circumstances.** An alien casually argues about a parking permit beside a god-sized disaster, and the permit dispute changes where somebody can dock. The joke affects decisions rather than decorating exposition.
2. **Status humiliation grows out of a character's insecurity.** Someone claims leadership, invents an award/title, receives a literal consequence, attempts to reframe failure, then gets undercut by the wrong person.
3. **Chaotic riff with a spine.** A ridiculous analogy leads to another person's correction, a defensive doubling-down, physical change and a punchline that irrevocably worsens their problem. Stop after the genuine peak.
4. **Mismatched moral registers.** Characters discuss dreadful crimes as logistics, budget, food, insurance or romance; another person may sincerely object. Let their choice carry consequence.
5. **Unexpected specificity and deranged institutions.** Exaggerated bureaucracy, deeply petty cosmology, weird body limitations, warped products, invented faiths or ceremonial nonsense can be funny if the character's reality and physical setup are consistent.
6. **Emotional whiplash.** A sincere sentence may follow filth, a quiet threat may beat a shouted gag, vulnerability may turn into an appalling personal demand. Absurdity does not cancel grief or compassion.
7. **Crossovers clash at incompatible genre logics.** A principled toy hero may confuse a corrupt captain; a penguin commando demands classified mission protocol from a house of fools. Protect originals' moral psychology even in an ugly setting.
8. **Fair TTS misdirection.** Mimir can delay a name, imply familiar silhouettes or deliver intimate corrective recollections, with visual/acoustic facts genuinely true.
9. **Pacing.** Build comedic escalation on distinct beats and active interruptions. Allow silence for payoff; cut endless shouting/repetition. A full 25–30 minute episode still has developed conflict; not every minute is a joke.
10. **Crude register is situational.** A person can be profane, lascivious, repulsive, vulgar, blasphemous, insult-driven, scatological, gallows-humorous, grotesque, sarcastic, sexually frank or ridiculously pompous. Not everyone speaks like Rick or Trover.

## Working profanity/raunch and insult language bank (NOT a script or quota)
General explosions: fuck, fucking hell, motherfucker, goddamn, goddammit, shit, bullshit, horseshit, piss, piss off, piss-poor, ass, asshole, asswipe, dumbass, jackass, shithead, shit-for-brains, son of a bitch, bastard, prick, dickhead, dipshit, douchebag, fuckwit, fuckface, fuckup, clusterfuck, shitshow, dumpster fire, piss-ant, cocky bastard, dickweed, twat, wanker, arsehole, knobhead, bollocks, bloody hell, shite, gobshite, bugger, numbnuts, fuckall, godforsaken, fuckery, screwup.
Crude imagery/complaints: balls-deep in trouble; a full-blown shit circus; got his head so far up his ass he can taste breakfast; bargain-bin bastard; a two-legged disaster with a mouth; a fucking landfill of bad ideas; couldn't find his ass with a map; that idea can eat shit; the world's worst fucking miracle; haunted colostomy of a plan; a wet-fart business model; professional ball-scratcher; a face like a punched lunch; bootleg bullshit; proud owner of a bankrupt brain; fart-knuckle; an overcooked turd of a scheme.
Raunchy adult words where character-real: horny, getting laid, hook up, shag, bang, screw, ass, tits, cock, dick, balls, nuts, libido, strip, cleavage, boner, hard-on, jerkoff, circle jerk, dry hump, horny bastard, fuckboy, thirsty, booty call, freaky, filthy-minded, pervy, slutty (as insulting term, not neutral identity); innuendo can be nastier than literal anatomical inventory.
Aggressive colorful speech patterns: impossible comparisons; body-function insults; religious/supernatural blasphemy; mock business jargon; perverse compliments; recurring humiliating nicknames earned inside relationship; interrupted corrections; half-finished rants; deliberately misplaced politeness; erotic double-entendre in adult contexts; hostile fake applause; distinct regional registers.
Selection rules: no cap on profanity count or intensity. **Do not turn the bank into an evenly distributed cuss-word checklist.** Differentiate Mimir's Celtic/Chicago cadence, Arthur's frontier sharpness, Chris's Theo-esque specificity, Tracy Morgan's indignation, Cartman's manipulative spite, Dutch's theatrical self-deception and Billy's honeyed menace. Do not casually use identity slurs to create false edginess, or sexualize minors. Approved vernacular and author-specified adult characters can still use harsh raw diction under their respective speech owners. Profanity is not a substitute for character agency or actually funny development.

## Scene application example (original calibration, not a screenplay requirement)
A con artist sells a "certified serpent-safe" ticket. Tracy shouts that he is NOT Busta Rhymes, interrupts the pitch to demand actual boots that fit, and shreds an invoice. Dr. Evil calls the invoice a declaration of war, while Cartman starts designing a merchandise royalty structure; Jimmy's grounded observation destroys their business logic. A real harbor witness then challenges the counterfeit safety claim, and the scam has material consequences. Work through the actual geography/individual agency first.

## Review standard
On each actual PLAY episode: point to specific revised passages where bizarre concepts affect motives, tonal collision is funny, character-specific swearing feels native, Mimir performs a fair narrative trick and silence enables payoff. No mandatory joke-per-page or scene/cameo requirement, and no scripted real-person voice clone.
